Abstract: Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) is an important modality in a wide range of imaging applications due to its versatile, long range and near all weather operating capabilities. However, Automatic Target Recognition (ATR) remains a challenging problem due to limited labelled data, the strong speckle in SAR images and the significant domain gap between SAR and more abundant optical imagery. In contrast, electro-optical (EO) imagery benefits from massive datasets, clearer visual structure and powerful foundation models. In this work, we investigate how vision foundation models trained on optical data can provide class level supervision for SAR classification.
  We propose a cross-modal EO to SAR prototype alignment framework in which a frozen EO encoder, based on a DINOv3 vision foundation model, is used to construct class level optical prototypes without requiring strict EO/SAR pairs. A SAR model is then trained to classify SAR images while aligning its embeddings to the corresponding EO class prototype. At inference time, the SAR model operates independently, without access to optical imagery. We evaluate our approach on the UNICORNv2 dataset, an EO and SAR dataset of civilian vehicles with heavily speckled images and severe class imbalance. EO prototype alignment improves SAR classification accuracy over frozen DINOv3, SAR only finetuning and unpaired distribution alignment baselines, and t-SNE visualizations provide qualitative evidence of clearer separation among classes in the trained SAR embedding space. These results suggest that optical vision foundation models, despite being trained on visible spectrum imagery, provide transferable information for SAR image classification, offering a practical method for using large scale pretrained vision foundation models across challenging sensing modalities.

</details>


### [204] [DroneGround: Open-Vocabulary Drone Payload Characterization Using Synthetic Data and Grounded Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07780)
*Ami Pandat,Rajasekhar Punna,Gopika Vinod,Rohit Shukla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DroneGround框架，用于无人机载荷的开放词汇表征与识别。使用Unreal Engine 5和Cosys-AirSim生成逼真合成数据集，通过YOLO26s检测器定位无人机并提取裁剪图像，再由LoRA微调的PaliGemma视觉语言模型生成语义描述，并通过遮挡体模块提供可解释的载荷定位。实验表明较传统封闭集检测器有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机载荷表征在远距离成像条件下面临严峻挑战：真实世界标注数据集稀缺，且部署时遭遇显著的分布偏移。现有方法将载荷表征视为封闭集目标检测问题，无法识别训练分布之外的新载荷类别，泛化能力受限。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 使用Unreal Engine 5和Cosys-AirSim生成逼真合成无人机载荷数据集；(2) YOLO26s检测器定位无人机并提取以无人机为中心的图像裁剪；(3) LoRA微调的PaliGemma视觉语言模型生成无人机及其载荷的语义描述，实现超越预定义类别的开放词汇表征；(4) 基于遮挡的定位模块通过识别生成描述所依赖的图像区域，提供可解释的载荷定位。

Result: 在合成和真实无人机图像上的大量实验表明：在合成到真实分布偏移下，F1分数从传统封闭集检测器的82.5%提升至96.3%；对未见载荷类别的泛化能力显著提升（80.4%对比42.7%的F1）。

Conclusion: DroneGround通过将封闭集检测范式转为开放词汇视觉语言表征，有效解决了无人机载荷表征中的分布偏移和未知类别泛化问题，同时遮挡体模块保证了结果的可解释性。

Abstract: Automated drone surveillance has become increasingly important for public safety, critical infrastructure protection,and restricted airspace monitoring. While existing vision-based systems achieve strong performance for drone detection and tracking, reliable payload characterization remains highly challenging under long-range imaging conditions due to limited availability of annotated real-world datasets, and substantial distribution shifts encountered during deployment. Existing approaches formulate payload characterization as a closed-set object detection problem, limiting their ability to recognize previously unseen payloads and generalize beyond the training distribution. To address these challenges, we generate a photorealistic synthetic drone-payload dataset using Unreal Engine 5 and Cosys-AirSim and propose DroneGround: Grounded Vision-Language Payload Characterization, a two-stage framework for robust open-vocabulary payload analysis. DroneGround first employs a YOLO26s detector to localize drones and extract drone-centric image crops, followed by a LoRA-fine-tuned PaliGemma vision-language model that generates seman- tic descriptions of the detected drones and their attached payloads, enabling open-vocabulary payload characterization beyond predefined categories. An occlusion-based grounding module further provides interpretable payload localization by identifying image regions responsible for the generated descriptions. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world drone imagery demonstrate that DroneGround substantially improves robustness under synthetic-to-real distribution shifts, outperforming a conventional closed-set payload detector by improving the F1-score from 82.5% to 96.3%, while achieving significantly better generalization to previously unseen payload categories (80.4%versus 42.7% F1). Dataset and code will be released upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [205] [VoT: Vision-of-Thought for Unified Multimodal Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07815)
*Jingxiang Sun,Chao Liao,Zhengxiong Luo,Chaorui Deng,Chen-lin Zhang,Junke Wang,Ceyuan Yang,Haoqi Fan,Weilin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Vision-of-Thought (VoT)框架，在视觉语言模型(VLM)和扩散Transformer(DiT)之间引入离散视觉思维层，使VLM作为多模态规划器生成代表高层视觉计划（如物体和布局）的离散VoT token，而非仅作为文本编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图系统采用"文本编码器加扩散解码器"范式，文本语义直接调制连续潜噪声，缺乏显式、可解释的中间表示来有效连接高层语言语义与低层视觉信号。

Method: 在VLM语义空间中训练专门的VoT分词器，采用闭环目标函数，结合VLM对齐损失、特征重建损失和向量量化损失。这些目标使token在语义上可被VLM理解，同时保留生成所需的视觉信息。

Result: 实验结果表明VoT提升了语义对齐能力，并为可解释和可控生成提供了结构化接口。

Conclusion: 通过引入离散视觉思维层，VoT突破了传统文本编码器范式的局限，实现了从高层语言语义到低层视觉信号的显式可解释桥梁，同时改善了生成的语义对齐与可控性。

Abstract: Current text-to-image systems typically employ a "text encoder plus diffusion decoder" paradigm, in which text semantics directly modulate continuous latent noise. Despite their success, these methods lack an explicit, interpretable intermediate representation that effectively bridges high-level linguistic semantics and low-level visual signals. In this paper, we propose Vision-of-Thought (VoT), a framework that introduces a discrete visual-thinking layer between vision-language models (VLMs) and diffusion transformers (DiTs). Instead of treating VLMs merely as text encoders, we use them as multimodal planners that generate discrete VoT tokens representing high-level visual plans, such as objects and layouts, before rendering pixels. We train a specialized VoT tokenizer in the VLM semantic space with a closed-loop objective that combines VLM alignment, feature reconstruction, and vector-quantization losses. These objectives make the tokens semantically readable by the VLM while preserving the visual information needed for generation. Experimental results demonstrate that VoT improves semantic alignment and provides a structured interface for interpretable and controllable generation.

</details>


### [206] [SAFIRE: Safety-Critical Benchmark for Fine-grained Fire and Smoke Understanding in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07823)
*Pengfei Li,Naufal Suryanto,Sicheng Zhang,Mohammad Alsharid,Muzammal Naseer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAFIRE，一个大规模火灾烟雾理解基准，包含83K图像和193K多选题，评估10个维度，发现现有MLLMs在安全关键推理上存在明显不足，且少量领域数据即可显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉语言任务上表现强劲，但其在安全关键场景（如火灾烟雾识别）中的可靠性研究不足。现有基准缺乏多样化的真实场景和情境感知评估，亟需一个全面、大规模的基准来系统评测MLLMs在此关键领域的表现。

Method: 构建SAFIRE基准：(1)包含83K张来自20个场景的带标题图像；(2)从9.7K图像子集生成193K个多项选择VQA问题，覆盖从基础感知到高阶推理的10个评估维度；(3)采用GPT-5.4辅助的多阶段验证流程结合MLLM多数投票确保注释质量；(4)评估10个开源MLLMs（8B-38B），并探索用领域数据微调视觉编码器的效果。

Result: 10个开源MLLM的平均准确率为61.9%，暴露出安全关键推理方面的显著不足。仅用7%领域数据适配视觉编码器，即可将火灾场景分类准确率从20.1%提升至64.5%，表明精心整理的少量数据可带来大幅性能提升。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs在火灾烟雾理解任务上的表现仍不理想，尤其在安全关键推理方面存在明显差距。高质量且精心整理的领域数据即使数量有限，也能显著提升模型性能，为未来安全关键视觉感知模型的改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong progress on vision-language tasks, yet their reliability in safety-critical settings remains underexplored. Fire-smoke understanding is central to public safety and disaster response, but most existing benchmarks lack diverse real-world scenarios and context-aware evaluation. We introduce SAFIRE, a large-scale benchmark for fire-smoke understanding in MLLMs, comprising 83K captioned images from 20 scenarios and 193K multiple-choice VQA (MCVQA) generated from a 9.7K-image subset, spanning 10 evaluation dimensions from basic perception to higher-order reasoning. A GPT-5.4-assisted multi-stage verification pipeline with MLLM majority voting ensures annotation quality. Evaluating ten open-source MLLMs (8B-38B) yields an average accuracy of 61.9%, exposing major gaps in safety-critical reasoning. We further show that adapting vision encoders with only 7% of our domain-specific data boosts fire-scene classification accuracy from 20.1% to 64.5%, indicating that carefully curated data can yield substantial gains even when data volume is limited. All datasets, models, and code are available at https://risys-lab.github.io/SAFIRE/.

</details>


### [207] [Are Image Generators Zero-Shot Perceivers? A Rigorous Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07884)
*Shangzhe Di,Zhaokai Wang,Weidi Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProbeGen基准，用于评估图像生成模型在零样本条件下执行视觉感知任务（包括单目深度估计、指代/推理分割和物体计数）的能力，对比了20个模型在11个公开基准上的表现，发现预训练图像生成器具备可测的零样本感知能力，但存在权衡：专用模型在分布内准确性和效率上更强，而生成模型在分布偏移下更鲁棒且具有更好的组合语义推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近的工作（如Vision Banana）表明，轻量级指令微调可以使图像生成器在多种视觉感知任务上达到最先进水平。受此启发，作者想探究图像生成器在零样本设置下能在公开视觉感知基准上走多远，以确立零样本生成式感知作为一个有意义的研究方向。

Method: 提出ProbeGen基准，将单目深度估计、指代/推理分割和物体计数重构为通过文本提示指定的条件生成任务；对20个模型（包括专有和开源图像生成器、专用感知模型以及MLLM）在11个公开基准上进行系统比较。

Result: 预训练图像生成器展现出可测量的零样本感知能力；专用模型在分布内准确性和效率上更优，而生成模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性和组合语义推理方面表现更好，存在明显的权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究有助于确立零样本生成式感知作为有意义的研究方向，为视觉生成与理解交叉领域的未来工作提供了基础。

Abstract: Recent work, such as Vision Banana, shows that lightweight instruction tuning can enable an image generator to achieve state-of-the-art performance across multiple visual perception tasks. Motivated by this perspective, we ask how far image generators can go on public visual perception benchmarks in a zero-shot setting. We introduce ProbeGen, a benchmark for zero-shot generative perception that casts monocular depth estimation, referring/reasoning segmentation, and object counting as conditional generation tasks specified through text prompts, and compares 20 models in total---including proprietary and open-weight image generators, specialist perception models, and MLLMs---across 11 published benchmarks. We observe that pretrained image generators show measurable zero-shot perceptual competence, but with a clear trade-off: specialist models remain stronger for in-distribution accuracy and efficiency, while generative models are often more robust under distribution shift and better at compositional semantic reasoning. We hope this study helps establish zero-shot generative perception as a meaningful research direction and provides a useful foundation for future work at the intersection of visual generation and understanding.

</details>


### [208] [JEDI: JEPA-to-Edge Distillation for Efficient Cropland Segmentation from Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07915)
*Kishor Kumar Bhaumik,Nicolas Roque dos Santos,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出JEDI（JEPA-to-Edge Distillation）两阶段框架，将大型I-JEPA ViT教师模型的知识蒸馏到紧凑的SegFormer学生模型，用于遥感分割的边缘部署。通过交叉架构投影与空间对齐实现表示对齐，并在任务适应阶段持续保持特征对齐。JEDI-B0在CalCROP21上达到68.0 mIoU（仅4.04M参数），比独立学生模型高16.0点，接近639M参数教师模型的70.0 mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉模型虽能提供有用的遥感分割表示，但参数量过大难以部署在卫星或边缘设备上。现有基于特征级的蒸馏方法往往假设教师和学生架构相似，且通常在任务训练开始时就停止特征对齐，限制了蒸馏效果。因此需要一种能够跨架构并持续对齐的蒸馏方法。

Method: JEDI采用两阶段蒸馏框架：第一阶段，通过跨架构投影和空间对齐，将学生终端的表示与I-JEPA教师的token空间对齐；第二阶段，联合优化三个目标——监督分割损失、温度缩放的响应蒸馏（response distillation）、以及在任务适应过程中持续进行的特征对齐。该方法支持跨架构蒸馏并保持特征对齐贯穿整个训练过程。

Result: 在CalCROP21数据集上，JEDI-B0达到68.0 mIoU（4.04M参数），比独立学生模型提升16.0个点，仅比639M参数的教师模型低2.0个点。对SegFormer B0（4.04M）、B1（14.33M）和B2（28M）三种学生规模，JEDI在所有变体上均优于响应、结构、通道和关系蒸馏等基线方法。

Conclusion: 持续表示对齐在激进压缩场景下尤为重要，能够在大幅减少模型规模和计算量的同时保持分割性能，为遥感分割模型在卫星或边缘端部署提供了有效的蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Large vision models provide useful representations for remote-sensing segmentation but are often too expensive for deployment at the satellite or field edge. Existing feature-level distillation methods also tend to assume similar teacher and student architectures and often stop feature alignment when task training begins. We introduce JEDI (JEPA-to-Edge Distillation), a two-stage framework that transfers representations from a large I-JEPA Vision Transformer teacher to a compact SegFormer student. First, JEDI aligns the student's terminal representation with the teacher's token space using cross-architecture projection and spatial alignment. It then jointly optimizes supervised segmentation, temperature-scaled response distillation, and persistent feature alignment throughout task adaptation. On CalCROP21, JEDI-B0 achieves 68.0 mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU) with 4.04M parameters, improving over the standalone student by 16.0 points and coming within 2.0 points of the 70.0 mIoU achieved by the 639M-parameter teacher. We evaluate SegFormer B0, B1, and B2 students with 4.04M, 14.33M, and 28M parameters, respectively. Across all three variants, JEDI consistently outperforms response-, structure-, channel-, and relational-distillation baselines under the same teacher-student setting. These results show that persistent representation alignment is especially valuable under aggressive compression, substantially reducing model size and computation while preserving segmentation performance.

</details>


### [209] [Poisson Image Denoising Using Minimax Concave and Reweighted $\ell_1$ Penalties: Nonblind and Blind Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07916)
*Reza Parvaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MCP（极小极大凹惩罚）和重加权分数阶梯度的图像去模糊方法，适用于泊松噪声下的盲和非盲图像恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像恢复在实际科学应用中面临挑战，特别是医学和天文图像受泊松噪声和模糊影响。当PSF未知时（盲问题）恢复尤为复杂。现有TV方法的惩罚函数选择是影响性能的关键因素，需要改进边缘保持能力。

Method: 采用重加权ℓ1范数正则化的分数阶导数来改善边缘保持；使用MCP（Minimax Concave Penalty）作为连续、稀疏促进且接近无偏的正则器，分别处理非盲和盲图像去模糊问题；基于ADMM设计高效数值算法并给出收敛性分析。

Result: 通过多种图像上的大量实验验证了所提算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于MCP和分数阶导数重加权正则化的ADMM算法在泊松噪声下的盲和非盲图像去模糊中表现出良好的性能。

Abstract: Images are important tools in various sciences. Despite the development of photo-taking tools, creating clear and image without noise remains challenging in practice. In particular, Poisson noise has an effect on medical and astronomical images, and reduces their quality. Additionally, blur is another factor that has an effect on image quality. The problem of image restoration becomes very complicated when we have no information about the Point Spread Function (PSF). These types of problems are known as blind case. However, in some images, such as some astronomical images, the type of PSF can be specified, and these types of problems are known as nonblind problems. Total Variation (TV) is a widely used method for solving such inverse problems, where the selection of the penalty function is the most critical factor that affects the method's performance. In this paper, to improve edge preservation, we employ a reweighted $\ell_1$-regularization of the fractional order derivative. Furthermore, we propose a nonblind and blind image deblurring approach under Poisson noise using the Minimax Concave Penalty (MCP), which is a continuous, sparsity promoting, and nearly unbiased regularizer. This formulation leads to a nonconvex optimization model. To solve the proposed model, we introduce an efficient numerical algorithm based on the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) and provide an analysis of its convergence. Finally, the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm are demonstrated through extensive experiments on various images.

</details>


### [210] [TDDN: Text-aligned Diffused DINO Network for Puzzle Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07937)
*Harsha Patnala,Debopriyo Banerjee,Ayush Sunil Munot,Somak Aditya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TDDN模型，通过融合DINOv3和CleanDIFT表示构建感知编码器（DiffusedDINO），并与RoBERTa-L对齐，在不使用大规模训练数据的情况下（仅约590K对齐对），在图像-文本检索上媲美CLIP，同时将密集预测精度提升三倍以上（ADE20K从5.20提升到18.11 mIoU）。此外引入Puzzle Perception数据集测试细粒度空间理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision Language Models (VLMs)基于CLIP风格的ViT骨干网络，在权衡中牺牲了细粒度视觉细节以换取高层语义信息，这种损失会向下游任务传播。结构化视觉推理（如图像谜题）需要细粒度感知能力，但当前VLMs在此方面存在明显不足。

Method: 1) 融合DINOv3和CleanDIFT表示构建感知编码器DiffusedDINO以恢复CLIP丢失的细粒度细节；2) 将DiffusedDINO与RoBERTa-L文本编码器对齐，得到文本对齐模型TDDN；3) 冻结骨干网络，仅用约590K对齐对进行训练；4) 引入Puzzle Perception数据集（包含分割和视觉问答任务）用于评估细粒度空间理解。

Result: TDDN在图像-文本检索上与CLIP持平，并在四项设置中的三项超越CLIP；在密集预测精度上比CLIP提升超过三倍（ADE20K: 5.20→18.11 mIoU, COCO-Stuff: 7.35→24.44 mIoU）；在分割基准上领先包括SigLIP 2在内的通用对比编码器；在Puzzle Perception数据集上分割精度是CLIP的两倍（11.04→22.51 mIoU）。

Conclusion: 通过融合自监督密集特征（DINOv3、CleanDIFT）与对比学习文本对齐，可以在不依赖大规模训练语料的情况下同时获得细粒度感知能力和文本对齐能力，证明感知细节的丢失并非VLM的固有局限，而是可被有效恢复的架构设计问题。

Abstract: Structured visual reasoning, such as image puzzles, demands fine-grained visual perception, an ability current Vision Language Models (VLMs) lack. VLMs built on CLIP-based ViT backbones trade fine-grained detail for high-level semantics, and we show this loss propagates downstream. To recover it, we fuse DINOv3 and CleanDIFT representations into a perception encoder (DiffusedDINO) and align it with RoBERTa-L, yielding a text-aligned model TDDN that preserves this perceptual advantage: with frozen backbones and only $\sim$590K alignment pairs, TDDN matches CLIP on image-text retrieval, surpassing it on three of four settings. It does so while more than tripling CLIP's dense-prediction accuracy (ADE20K 5.20 $\to$ 18.11 mIoU, COCO-Stuff 7.35 $\to$ 24.44), despite CLIP's massive training corpus. TDDN leads on segmentation benchmarks among general-purpose contrastive encoders, including SigLIP$\,$2. We further introduce Puzzle Perception, a segmentation and visual question answering dataset that probes fine-grained spatial understanding, on which TDDN doubles CLIP's segmentation accuracy (11.04 $\to$ 22.51 mIoU).

</details>


### [211] [Bottom-up Modeling of Repeated Elements via Single Image Analysis-by-Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07939)
*Syrine Kalleli,Alexei A. Efros,Mathieu Aubry*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅从单张图像中以自底向上方式发现重复元素的方法，无需标注数据集、多图像集合或分割掩码，仅需一个粗略的尺度先验，通过重建目标学习可调图像空间原型，在FSC-147数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重复元素发现方法依赖大规模标注数据集、精心整理的多图像集合或对象分割掩码，而人类从单张图像即可识别重复模式。作者希望探索仅凭单张图像能否以完全自底向上的方式学习有意义的对象模型。

Method: 该方法通过重建目标学习一个可调（tunable）的图像空间原型，该原型代表重复元素的模式。在粗尺度先验的引导下，模型能够从单张图像中识别并合成一致的重复对象实例，无需任何外部先验知识。

Result: 在FSC-147数据集的116张真实图像上进行了实验，方法成功学习了连贯的元素模型并捕获了具有挑战性图像上的类内变化。定性结果显示，相比经典分解、联合对齐和3D对象建模方法，该方法在重建质量和可解释分解方面表现更优，同时保持简单的2D公式。

Conclusion: 有意义的对象发现可以从单张图像学习中涌现，该工作证明了在无需大量标注或多图像监督的条件下，模型仍能发现和建模重复结构，这为无监督视觉学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: We address the problem of discovering repeated elements from a single image. In contrast to existing approaches that depend on large annotated datasets, curated multi-image collections, or object segmentation masks, we show that a single image can suffice to learn a meaningful object model in a completely bottom-up fashion, without any prior knowledge beyond a coarse scale prior. Our method learns a tunable image-space prototype of the repeated elements through a reconstruction objective, enabling the model to identify and synthesize consistent object instances within the same image. Experiments on 116 real images from the FSC-147 dataset demonstrate that our method successfully learns coherent element models and captures intra-category variation on challenging images. Qualitative results reveal superior reconstructions and interpretable decompositions compared to classical decomposition, joint alignment, and 3D object modeling methods, while maintaining a simple 2D formulation. These results suggest that meaningful object discovery can emerge from single image learning alone.

</details>


### [212] [ReactVAU: A Slow-Fast Decoupled Framework for Streaming Video Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07941)
*Chia-Hui Chen,Shih-Ying Yeh,Fu-En Yang,Min-Hung Chen,Shang-Hong Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReactVAU，一个慢-快解耦框架，用于实时流式视频异常理解（VAU）。它通过轻量级快速检测模块（基于空间网格折叠SGF）持续过滤异常，利用异常感知持久记忆（AAPM）保护关键视觉线索免受时间衰减，并由仅在可疑事件发生时被唤醒的重型慢推理模块进行语义验证和因果描述，从而在严格流式约束下实现竞争性的异常检测与因果推理性能，同时大幅提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAU方法依赖离线推理和全局时间采样，违反因果性且无法部署于实时监控流；而通用流式视频模型在记忆压缩时会稀释罕见的瞬时异常，且常对长段正常间隔统一调用重型多模态大语言模型，造成计算浪费。

Method: ReactVAU由三个协同组件构成：1) 基于空间网格折叠（SGF）的轻量级快速检测模块，用于连续的异常过滤；2) 异常感知持久记忆（AAPM），保护关键视觉线索免受时间衰减；3) 重型慢推理模块，在正常流期间保持休眠，仅在可疑事件触发时才被唤醒，用于语义验证和因果描述。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，ReactVAU在严格流式约束下运行，同时在异常检测和因果推理上取得竞争性表现，并通过最小化重型MLLM调用显著提升计算效率。

Conclusion: ReactVAU通过快慢解耦架构弥合了实时流式处理与视频异常理解之间的鸿沟，验证了在不牺牲推理质量的前提下实现高效、因果的流式异常理解是可行的。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose ReactVAU, a Slow-Fast Decoupled Framework for real-time streaming Video Anomaly Understanding (VAU). Existing VAU methods rely on offline inference with global temporal sampling, which violates causality and prevents deployment in live surveillance streams. Conversely, general streaming video models satisfy causal access but dilute rare transient anomalies during memory compression and often invoke heavyweight MLLMs uniformly over long normal intervals. React VAU addresses this gap with three synergistic components: a lightweight Fast Detection Module based on Spatial Grid Folding (SGF) for continuous anomaly filtering; an Anomaly-Aware Persistent Memory (AAPM) that protects critical visual cues from temporal decay; and a heavyweight Slow Reasoning Module that remains dormant during normal streams and is awakened only by suspicious events for semantic verification and causal description. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ReactVAU operates under strict streaming constraints while simultaneously achieving competitive performance in both anomaly detection and causal reasoning, alongside significantly enhanced computational efficiency by minimizing heavyweight MLLM invocations. Project page is available at https://huiyuiui.github.io/React_VAU/

</details>


### [213] [TaskGuard: Task-Conditioned Restoration Utility for Risk-Aware Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08011)
*Vung Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TaskGuard，一个用于冷冻恢复与检测管道的后置控制器，用于预测图像恢复是否对下游目标检测任务有益，而非仅以视觉质量为准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像恢复通常被预先应用于恶劣条件下的目标检测，但视觉上改善的图像并不总能提升下游任务性能。作者希望研究这种不匹配，并提出恢复效用预测问题：给定退化图像及其候选恢复结果，应使用恢复还是保留原始观测？

Method: TaskGuard通过表征恢复残差与检测器灵敏度的交互来预测干预是否任务有益。利用精确的区域反事实揭示图像内部效用异质性，采用可部署的伪梯度保留统计可靠的方向信息；通过特征组消融表明任务条件化证据贡献超出检测器响应和残差统计的信息。预测器仅在Gaussian退化上训练并冷冻，然后迁移到未见过的运动模糊、雨和散焦场景。

Result: 在未见过的退化家族中，TaskGuard减少了54.2%（家族宏平均）的负损失干预和37.0%（合并）的每图像检测退化，同时保留了Always-Restore COCO AP的98.8%。在自然降雨DAWN数据集上，减少了97.9%的负损失干预，同时保留了去雨所获AP改进的77.8%。

Conclusion: 结果表明恢复效用是特定干预的任务条件化属性，而非仅由图像外观决定。

Abstract: Image restoration is commonly applied before object detection under adverse conditions, yet a visually improved image need not improve the downstream task. We study this mismatch as restoration utility prediction: given a degraded image and its candidate restoration, should the restoration be used or should the original observation be preserved? We introduce TaskGuard, a post-hoc controller for frozen restoration and detection pipelines. TaskGuard characterizes the realized restoration residual through its interaction with detector sensitivity and predicts whether the intervention is task-beneficial. Exact regional counterfactuals reveal substantial within-image utility heterogeneity, while a deployable pseudo-gradient preserves statistically reliable directional information. Feature-group ablation further shows that task-conditioned evidence contributes information beyond detector-response and residual statistics. The TaskGuard utility predictor is trained only on Gaussian degradation and frozen before final evaluation, then transferred to unseen motion blur, rain, and defocus. Across these unseen families, TaskGuard reduces lossnegative interventions by 54.2% (family macro) and practical per-image detection deteriorations by 37.0% (pooled), while preserving 98.8% of the Always-Restore COCO AP. On natural-rain DAWN, it reduces loss-negative interventions by 97.9% while retaining 77.8% of the AP improvement obtained by deraining. These results support restoration utility as a task-conditioned property of the specific intervention rather than image appearance alone.

</details>


### [214] [A Black-Box Adversarial Attack on Human Pose Estimation and Keypoint-Based Action Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08013)
*Kacper Mroczek,Michal Kepski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于决策的黑盒攻击方法OKS Attack，利用Object Keypoint Similarity (OKS)作为攻击反馈信号，直接针对人类姿态估计中的关键点空间结构进行攻击，而非包围框级别的相似度。实验表明该方法能有效降低姿态估计质量并显著降低下游动作识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗攻击研究多针对视觉目标跟踪（使用IoU等包围框度量），但人体姿态估计输出的是关键点配置而非包围框，使用框级相似度难以衡量姿态退化。因此需要一种针对关键点空间结构的攻击反馈信号来有效攻击人体姿态估计和关键点动作识别模型。

Method: 提出OKS Attack，一种决策级（decision-based）黑盒攻击方法。核心是利用Object Keypoint Similarity (OKS)作为攻击的反馈信号，直接针对人体姿态的空间结构（关键点配置）进行扰动优化，而非依赖包围框重叠度量。

Result: 在Penn Action数据集上，OKS Attack能一致降低各姿态估计器的姿态质量，平均OKS下降范围为0.0802至0.1494。在下游跨数据集动作识别评估中，攻击使准确率降低6.18至13.86个百分点，并优于查询匹配的随机噪声扰动。该攻击对自上而下（top-down）和单阶段姿态估计模型均有效。

Conclusion: OKS Attack证明了以关键点相似度作为攻击反馈信号可以有效攻击人体姿态估计模型，填补了该领域对抗攻击研究的空白，且其攻击效果可传导至下游动作识别任务，代码已开源供进一步研究。

Abstract: Human pose estimation and keypoint-based action recognition models are increasingly deployed as components of video understanding pipelines, yet their vulnerability to adversarial attacks remains insufficiently studied. Temporally coherent black-box attacks have been previously studied in visual object tracking, where the attack feedback can be defined using bounding-box overlap measures such as Intersection over Union (IoU). However, human pose estimation produces keypoint configurations rather than enclosing boxes, making box-level similarity poorly suited for measuring pose degradation. We propose OKS Attack, a decision-based black-box attack that uses Object Keypoint Similarity (OKS) as the attack feedback signal, directly targeting the spatial structure of human poses rather than their enclosing boxes. Experiments on the Penn Action dataset show that OKS Attack consistently reduces pose quality across evaluated pose estimators, with mean OKS decreases ranging from 0.0802 to 0.1494. In a downstream cross-dataset action-recognition evaluation, the attack reduces accuracy by 6.18 to 13.86 percentage points and outperforms query-matched random-noise perturbations. The attack is effective across both top-down and single-stage pose estimation models. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/KacperM33/OKS_attack

</details>


### [215] [Flexible Motion Generation from Language and Style References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08032)
*Kai Weixian Lan,Bodie Criswell,Briana Fedkiw,Zhan Zhang,Joseph Teran,Daniel Holden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexMoGen是一个结合自然语言描述和动作风格参考的灵活人体动作合成框架，通过变分风格编码器和文本到动作的潜在扩散模型联合预训练，无需风格监督即可支持长时间、时变及多风格的动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本提示擅长定义语义内容，但难以捕捉精细的风格细节（如时序、肢体 articulation 和表现力动态）。风格示例片段能补充这些细微动作特征，现有方法依赖离散风格标签且无法泛化到长序列或多风格生成。

Method: FlexMoGen联合预训练一个无需风格监督的变分风格编码器和一个文本到动作的潜在扩散模型，通过轻量级适配模块调制动作风格，集成高效相对位置编码，并同时在风格化和非风格化数据集上训练。

Result: 实验表明FlexMoGen在内容保真度和风格反映之间取得了最佳平衡，并能泛化到未见过的文本-风格组合。

Conclusion: FlexMoGen通过统一的架构设计，克服了传统方法在风格标签依赖和长序列/多风格生成方面的局限，为文本和风格双条件的人体动作合成提供了更灵活、更可控的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce FlexMoGen, a novel framework for flexible human motion synthesis conditioned on both natural language descriptions and motion style references. Text prompts are effective at defining semantic content, but they are often limited in capturing fine-grained style details such as timing, limb articulation, and expressive dynamics. A style example clip supplements the text by conveying these nuanced motion characteristics directly, enabling the model to preserve high-level intent while reproducing the desired stylistic traits. Given a text prompt and a style example clip, FlexMoGen generates high-quality motions that preserve semantic content while faithfully reflecting the target style, offering users greater control over the animation generation process. Unlike prior methods that rely on discrete style labels and do not generalize to long or multi-style generation, FlexMoGen learns a variational style encoder without style supervision and supports long, time-varying, multi-style synthesis. Our framework jointly pre-trains the style encoder and a text-to-motion latent diffusion model within a unified architecture, modulating motion style through a lightweight adaptation module. It integrates an efficient relative positional encoding scheme and is trained on both stylized and non-stylized datasets, enabling strong generalization to unseen text-style combinations. Experiments show that FlexMoGen achieves the best balance between content fidelity and style reflection.

</details>


### [216] [SAFER-Activities: A Dataset for Smart Assessment of Fall Events and Routine Activities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08038)
*Diwas Lamsal,Pramod Wickramatilake,Jednipat Moonrinta,Mongkol Ekpanyapong,Matthew N. Dailey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAFER-Activities数据集，包含超过66小时、85310个动作实例、30类动作类别的多摄像头视频数据，专用于跌倒检测与身体活动监测（含轮椅场景子集），并配备帧级标注。作者通过2D/3D骨架模型、冻结骨干RGB模型及多模态融合策略进行基准测试，发现骨架模型在域偏移下泛化最佳；融合冻结RGB特征可提升域内识别但在分布外场景下降；跨数据集评估表明模型能良好迁移到未见环境与外部跌倒数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作识别数据集多为片段级别（clip-based），缺乏帧级标注，无法支持在线（实时流式）动作识别需求，尤其在跌倒等需要及时干预的关键场景中。这一空白阻碍了智能健康监测系统对行动不便人群的精准监护发展。

Method: 构建SAFER-Activities多模态数据集，包含多摄像头采集的66+小时视频、85310个动作实例、30类动作的帧级标注及轮椅场景专用子集。基准测试涵盖2D和3D骨架模型、冻结骨干的RGB模型、以及多模态融合策略，并设置实验室内、分布外、跨数据集三组评测集。

Result: 骨架模型在域偏移下泛化效果最佳；将冻结RGB特征与骨架流融合可提升域内识别（较基线CNN1D），在轮椅子集上提升最明显，但会损害分布外性能。跨数据集与定性评估证实SAFER-Activities训练出的模型能有效迁移至未见环境和外部跌倒数据。

Conclusion: SAFER-Activities填补了帧级在线动作识别数据集的空白，为跌倒检测和活动监测研究提供高质量基准。研究揭示了骨架与RGB融合在域内与域外场景间的权衡，为实际部署中的模型选择提供指导。数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Smart healthcare monitoring systems require precise action recognition to ensure well-being and timely intervention in critical situations such as falls, particularly for mobility-challenged individuals. Existing datasets are often clip-based, lacking the frame-level detail needed to recognize actions online, as they unfold. To address this, we introduce SAFER-Activities, a dataset for fall detection and physical activity monitoring, with a dedicated subset for wheelchair use scenarios. It comprises over 66 hours of video data captured by multiple cameras, with 85,310 action instances and frame-level annotations for 30 action classes. We benchmark action recognition on SAFER-Activities with 2D and 3D skeleton models, RGB models with frozen backbones, and multimodal fusion strategies, and evaluate on in-lab, out-of-distribution, and cross-dataset test sets. Skeleton-based models generalize best under domain shift; fusing frozen RGB features with the skeleton stream improves in-domain recognition over the baseline CNN1D, most clearly on the wheelchair subset, but degrades out of distribution. Cross-dataset and qualitative evaluations confirm that models trained on SAFER-Activities transfer well to unseen environments and external fall data. To support research on robust fall detection and activity monitoring, we release the dataset and code at https://safer-activities.github.io/.

</details>


### [217] [MamMA: A Mamba-Based Pedestrian Trajectory Prediction Algorithm Considering Occupancy Map and Pedestrian Awareness States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08041)
*Juncen Long,Xiaofeng Jin,Gianluca Bardaro,Simone Mentasti,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MamMA，一种基于Mamba的行人轨迹预测算法，同时利用激光雷达构建的占据栅格地图和车载视觉传感器提供的行人感知状态信息，在多个数据集上优于现有最先进算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行人轨迹预测算法多依赖俯视图像提取障碍物信息，但移动机器人实际使用LiDAR构建局部占据地图而非俯视图像；同时车载视觉传感器提供的自我中心视角图像中含有行人的细粒度行为信息未被充分利用。

Method: MamMA将占据地图分块提取障碍物特征生成地图特征；将行人感知状态分类并纳入考虑（因为感知状态影响行人的感知和速度）；提出基于Mamba的模型，融合不同类型特征来预测行人未来轨迹。

Result: 在STCrowd、SiT、JRDB、ETH和UCY五个数据集上进行实验，MamMA在平均位移误差（ADE）和最终位移误差（FDE）上均优于最先进的算法。

Conclusion: 通过有效融合LiDAR占据地图特征和行人感知状态，Mamba架构能够显著提升行人轨迹预测精度，为移动机器人在人机共融环境中的安全导航提供更好的支持。

Abstract: Many pedestrian trajectory prediction algorithms have been proposed to improve the safety of navigation for mobile robots working in human-robot coexistence environments. Some pedestrian trajectory prediction algorithms extract information about obstacles near pedestrians from top-down view images to improve the accuracy of trajectory prediction. However, mobile robots typically create local occupancy maps using LiDAR, rather than top-down view images. Meanwhile, the vision sensors on board robots provide egocentric view images, which contain fine-grained behavioral information about the pedestrians near the robot. To better use the information collected by LiDAR and on-board vision sensors, we propose MamMA, a Mamba-based pedestrian trajectory prediction algorithm considering occupancy maps and pedestrian awareness states. MamMA divides the occupancy map by patches and extracts obstacle features from each patch to create map features. Pedestrian awareness states are divided and considered, as some studies show that awareness states affect the perception and speed of pedestrians. Furthermore, a Mamba-based model is proposed to predict the future trajectories of pedestrians based on different types of features. Experiments on the STCrowd, SiT, JRDB, ETH, and UCY datasets show that MamMA achieves better average displacement error and final displacement error than the state-of-the-art algorithms.

</details>


### [218] [A Quantitative Evaluation Framework for Temporal Explainability in Echocardiographic Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08043)
*Jiyoo Noh,Jonathan H. Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于评估超声心动图视频分割模型中Grad-CAM时间可解释性的定量框架，包含时序一致性、显著性运动、解剖重叠和时间重叠四个互补指标。实验发现，虽然中间层ConvLSTM解释的稳定性较低，但时间瓶颈层比编码器瓶颈层更稳定，且解码器最终解释与2D U-Net相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在超声心动图视频分割中已达到最先进水平，越来越多模型融入时间信息，但时间可解释性的定量评估仍基本未被探索，现有逐帧解释指标无法区分中间层解释变化是反映有意义的时间特征演化还是解释不稳定。

Method: 提出一个定量评估框架，使用四个互补指标（时间一致性、显著性运动、解剖重叠、时间重叠）评估Grad-CAM解释。在EchoNet-Dynamic数据集上，对比基线2D U-Net与在不同时间步长下训练的ConvLSTM U-Net模型，并对编码器瓶颈、时间瓶颈和解码器三个层面的解释进行系统比较。

Result: 所有模型的分割性能相当，但中间ConvLSTM解释的显著性一致性和质心运动显著低于最终预测解释。时间瓶颈解释在所有时间步长上比编码器瓶颈解释更稳定，而最终ConvLSTM解码器3解释与2D U-Net大体相当。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了时间可解释性的初步定量框架，证实传统逐帧指标不足以判断中间层解释变化的原因，并激励开发显式考虑医学视频模型中演化表示的时间感知可解释性AI方法。

Abstract: Deep learning has achieved state-of-the-art performance in echocardiographic video segmentation, with an increasing number of models incorporating temporal information. However, quantitative evaluation of temporal explainability remains largely unexplored. We propose a quantitative framework for evaluating Grad-CAM explanations using four complementary metrics measuring temporal consistency, saliency motion, anatomical overlap, and temporal overlap. Using EchoNet-Dynamic, we compare a baseline 2D U-Net with ConvLSTM U-Net models trained across multiple temporal strides. While segmentation performance remained comparable across all models, intermediate ConvLSTM explanations exhibited substantially lower saliency consistency and greater centroid motion than final prediction explanations. Temporal Bottleneck explanations were significantly more stable than Encoder Bottleneck explanations across all strides, while final ConvLSTM Decoder3 explanations were broadly comparable to those of the 2D U-Net. Importantly, conventional frame-wise explanation metrics cannot determine whether variation in intermediate explanations reflects meaningful temporal feature evolution or explanation instability. These findings establish a preliminary quantitative framework for temporal explainability and motivate temporal-aware XAI methods that explicitly account for evolving representations in medical video models.

</details>


### [219] [RFS-UNet: Decoder-Conditioned High-Resolution Skip Recalibration for Bone-Selective DRR Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08044)
*Xiaoyang Li,Yixuan Liu,Yuan Chai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RFS-UNet，通过池化的编码器特征和对齐的解码器统计量对高分辨率跳连（512²和256²）进行有界残差通道重标定，验证解码器状态对高分辨率特征复用的价值。在种子2026匹配对比中，RFS较仅编码器自校准的Self-RFS提升验证PSNR 0.254 dB；三种子锁定测试PSNR从33.225±0.048升至33.537±0.128 dB，在179/200例CT中降低MAE，平均MAE下降3.91%，参数仅增0.117%、Conv2d运算增1.169%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨选择性DRR合成依赖高分辨率编码器细节，但静态跳连无法根据解码器表示演化来条件化复用。作者质疑解码器状态是否能提供超出编码器自校准的额外信息，以改善高分辨率跳连复用。

Method: RFS-UNet在512²和256²跳连处，利用池化的编码器特征和对齐的解码器统计量进行有界残差通道重标定，网络骨干结构保持不变，以此隔离并评估解码器条件化带来的增益。

Result: 在种子2026匹配设置下，RFS较Self-RFS提升验证PSNR 0.254 dB；三种子锁定测试中PSNR由33.225±0.048升至33.537±0.128 dB；在179/200例未见CT中MAE降低，平均MAE减少3.91%；仅增加0.117%参数和1.169%计数的Conv2d运算。

Conclusion: 解码器状态是控制配对投影合成中高分辨率特征复用的有效条件化信号，支持在高分辨率跳连中引入解码器感知的重标定机制。

Abstract: Bone-selective digitally reconstructed radiograph (DRR) synthesis depends on high-resolution encoder detail, yet static skips cannot condition reuse on the evolving decoder representation. We ask whether decoder state adds useful information beyond encoder-only self-recalibration for high-resolution skip reuse. RFS-UNet uses pooled encoder and aligned decoder statistics for bounded residual channel recalibration at the 512^2 and 256^2 skips, leaving the backbone unchanged. In the matched seed-2026 comparison isolating decoder conditioning, RFS raises validation PSNR by 0.254 dB over Self-RFS. Across three seeds, locked-test PSNR rises from 33.225+/-0.048 to 33.537+/-0.128 dB; RFS lowers MAE in 179/200 held-out CT cases and reduces mean MAE by 3.91%. It adds 0.117% parameters and 1.169% counted Conv2d operations. These results support decoder state as a useful conditioning signal for high-resolution feature reuse in controlled paired projection synthesis.

</details>


### [220] [BrachistoneLR: A Brachistochrone-Inspired Learning-Rate Schedule and a Controlled Benchmark of Scheduling Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08069)
*Md. Sadekur Rahman Roni,Md. Jalal uddin Chowdhury,Moutusi Dash Nimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究BrachistoneLR学习率调度策略，发现它本质上是半周期设为E-1的余弦退火，与标准余弦退火仅在最后epoch有E^-2量级的微小差异。通过72次运行、3个数据集和4种架构的严格控制实验，发现平滑下降的调度优于常数学习率和基于日历的衰减，但BrachistoneLR、余弦退火和warmup-cosine之间在统计上不可区分（差异小于0.06精度点），表明调度形状比参数化更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习率调度策略大多是启发式的，且此前的研究比较时往往同时改变架构、数据集和预算，难以清晰归因性能差异。作者希望在一个控制严格的框架下公平比较各调度的真实效果。

Method: 1) 从数学上推导BrachistoneLR（最速降线映射）等于半周期为E-1的余弦退火，并证明其与标准余弦退火的差随E^-2衰减；2) 在固定优化器、数据管道和评估协议条件下，对6种调度在3个数据集和4种架构上进行72次实验对比。

Result: 平滑从峰值向地板值下降的调度在所有难度等级上均优于常数学习率和基于日历的衰减，在CIFAR-10上优势达2.5个准确率点。BrachistoneLR、余弦退火与warmup-cosine之间的差距仅0.06精度点和0.17的平均排名，单种子实验无法区分。BrachistoneLR在残差网络上最优且CIFAR-10均值最高。

Conclusion: 调度曲线的形状比其具体参数化更重要；使用平滑调度的决策比在平滑调度之间做选择更重要；末端速率差异仅在短训练周期下值得关注。

Abstract: The learning-rate schedule is a consequential choice in training deep networks, yet the policies in common use are heuristic, and published comparisons are hard to read, because architecture, dataset, and budget tend to vary alongside the schedule. We study BrachistoneLR, a schedule built by mapping the vertical coordinate of the brachistochrone, the curve of fastest descent under gravity, onto the range between a peak and a floor rate. Expanding the definition shows it to be cosine annealing with the half-period set to E - 1 instead of E, the configuration a standard implementation gives when its period argument is one less than the number of epochs. The rate therefore reaches its floor at the last epoch trained rather than one epoch later, and we show this difference decays as E^-2, making it a short-horizon effect. We then benchmark six schedules over 72 runs on three image classification datasets (MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10) and four architecture families (fully connected, convolutional, recurrent, residual), fixing the optimizer, data pipeline, and evaluation protocol so that only the schedule varies. Schedules that fall smoothly from peak to floor beat the constant rate and calendar-based decay by margins that grow with task difficulty, reaching 2.5 points of dataset mean on CIFAR-10. Within that leading group, BrachistoneLR, cosine annealing, and warmup-cosine lie within 0.06 accuracy points and 0.17 of a mean rank, which one seed per configuration cannot separate. BrachistoneLR is best on both residual networks and has the highest CIFAR-10 mean, and it sets no milestones, decay factor, warmup length, or restart period. We conclude that the shape of a schedule matters more than its parameterization, that the choice of whether to use a smooth schedule matters more than the choice among them, and that the terminal-rate distinction is worth attention only over short horizons.

</details>


### [221] [VI-Bench: Benchmarking Prompt Inversion from AIGC Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08079)
*Wulin Xie,Rui Zhao,Kecen Li,Xiujin Liu,Bokang Zhang,Zheng Liu,Xinwen Hou,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VI-Bench基准，用于评估视频生成中的提示词反演能力。该基准基于1610万真实用户提示和900个经过人工验证的AIGC视频构建，涵盖三种难度递增的设置和五个生成关键维度（主体、动作、场景、风格、相机）。对18个VLM模型的评估显示，即使最强模型的反演分数也仅达0.632，揭示视频提示反演是一项被低估且独特的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准无法衡量提示词反演能力——它们只关注描述可见内容，而可回放的提示词必须恢复生成相关的控制参数（如风格、相机行为）以复现视频。这一能力对创意复用、编辑以及评估提示词泄露风险都至关重要。

Method: 构建VI-Bench基准：从1610万真实用户提示和900个人工验证的AIGC视频出发，设计三种递进难度的设置（单次语义接地、风格与相机行为控制、多次组合反演），评估五个生成关键维度。使用Inversion Score衡量提示词级对齐和视频级保真度，对2个专有和16个开源VLM模型进行系统评估。

Result: 18个VLM模型在VI-Bench上表现有限：最强模型Inversion Score仅达0.632；性能随控制需求和多次推理复杂度增加而急剧下降；模型常生成看似合理的提示词，但其再生成的视频与参考视频存在偏差。

Conclusion: 视频提示词反演是一个独特且评估不足的能力，要求模型将视觉理解转化为可回放稳定的生成控制，现有VLM在这方面的能力存在显著局限。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation have made prompt-based control increasingly central to AIGC video generation. Prompts specify what a video should depict and how it should be represented, controlling factors such as visual style or camera behavior. Understanding this recoverability is important both for creative reuse and editing, and for assessing prompt leakage risks. However, existing video understanding benchmarks do not measure this capability: a caption may describe what is visible, but a replayable prompt must recover the generation-relevant controls needed to reproduce the video. To address this gap, we introduce VI-Bench, a benchmark built from 16.1 million real-user prompts and 900 human-verified AIGC videos. VI-Bench spans three progressively harder settings, namely single-shot semantic grounding, control over style and camera behavior, and multi-shot compositional inversion, and evaluates five generation-critical dimensions: subject, action, scene, style, and camera. We evaluate 18 representative VLMs, including 2 proprietary and 16 open-source models on VI-Bench, using an Inversion Score that measures prompt-level alignment with the original prompt and video-level fidelity of the regenerated video. The results reveal substantial limitations: even the strongest model achieves only 0.632 on Inversion Score, performance degrades sharply as samples require richer control and multi-shot reasoning, and models often produce plausible prompts whose regenerated videos deviate from the reference. These findings show that video prompt inversion is a distinct and under-evaluated capability requiring models to transform visual understanding into replay-stable generative control.

</details>


### [222] [Marigold V2: Revisiting Diffusion Transformers for Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08084)
*Igor Pavlovic,Thiemo Wandel,Anton Obukhov,Luca Bartolomei,Andrey Davydov,Fabio Tosi,Matteo Poggi,Sabine Süsstrunk,Dengxin Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Marigold V2，通过重新审视并改进Marigold技术，将基于扩散Transformer（DiT）的图像生成与编辑模型改造为最先进的单目深度估计器，支持单步推理和量化，显著提升了深度图的锐利度、清晰度和跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度估计是重要但高度不适定的计算机视觉任务，现有模型在域外输入上泛化能力不足，且难以生成锐利、细节丰富的深度图。

Method: 1) 从预训练的多步flow-matching模型中实现单步推理，并在需要时进行量化，在保持模型容量的同时降低运行成本；2) 将模型内部表示与从真值提取的语义特征对齐；3) 提出基于新型Sinkhorn损失的2阶段微调协议，以修复朴素训练产生的伪影。

Result: 在KITTI和ETH3D数据集上，AbsRel相较于此前最优方法提升了16-26%；能解析毛发、树叶和细薄边缘等此前模型无法处理的细节；在其他稠密回归任务（表面法线估计、固有图像分解）上也达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: Marigold V2通过表示对齐与Sinkhorn-based两阶段微调策略，成功将大规模扩散生成模型高效转化为高质量、强泛化的稠密回归模型，证明了该范式的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation is a ubiquitous yet highly ill-posed computer vision task, with downstream applications in scene reconstruction, computational photography, and robotics, among others. Despite the field's maturity, recent models still struggle to generalize to out-of-distribution inputs and to produce sharp and detailed depth maps. In this paper, we revisit Marigold, a set of techniques for repurposing modern image generation and editing models, powered by the diffusion transformer (DiT) architecture, into state-of-the-art monocular depth estimators. Our recipes target single-step inference from pretrained multi-step flow-matching models, with quantization where needed, preserving model capacity while remaining cheap to run. We analyze the artifacts of naive training and identify two effective remedies: aligning the model's internal representations with semantic features extracted from ground-truth, and adopting a 2-stage fine-tuning protocol built around a novel Sinkhorn-based loss. The results are crisper, cleaner depth maps that generalize well out-of-distribution, with 16-26% improvement in AbsRel over the previous best on KITTI and ETH3D. Qualitatively, our model resolves fur, foliage, and hair-thin edges that have eluded prior models. Furthermore, Marigold V2 achieves state-of-the-art results when applied to other dense regression tasks, such as surface normals estimation and intrinsic image decomposition. Project website: https://hf.co/spaces/huawei-bayerlab/marigold-v2-web

</details>


### [223] [SynthGait-19K: A Physically Grounded Synthetic Video Dataset for Gait Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08108)
*Soroush Mehraban,Xin Lei Lin,Vida Adeli,Majid Mirmehdi,Amirhossein Dadashzadeh,Clint Hansen,Andrea Iaboni,Babak Taati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SynthGait-19k，一个基于物理的合成视频数据集，包含19,272个行走视频，来源于437名受试者的6,427个MoCap序列，并配对了六个步态参数标注。文章开发了Gait2Vid方法统一异构MoCap记录并合成多样化RGB行走视频，引入GaitXFormer作为直接RGB参考模型，验证了合成监督可有效迁移到真实视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单目视频准确估计临床意义上步态参数对可扩展的移动能力评估很重要，但现有数据集存在规模小、视角受限、视觉多样性不足等限制，阻碍了该领域进展。因此需要一个大规模、多样化且带可靠标注的步态数据集。

Method: 1) 构建SynthGait-19k数据集，通过Gait2Vid方法将异构MoCap记录统一到SMPL表示，并合成多样化的RGB行走视频，支持可控视角和场景外观。2) 评估生成视频与步态运动学的一致性，并用测力台数据进行步态事件验证。3) 引入GaitXFormer作为直接RGB参考模型，并对比多种方法（直接RGB、姿态、生物力学、人体网格恢复）。4) 分析视角、训练数据规模及合成到真实的域迁移影响。

Result: 合成监督能有效迁移到真实视频，适用于GaitXFormer和基于姿态的架构；空间步态参数对视觉域迁移更敏感；改进的HMR重建不必然转化为下游步态估计性能的提升。

Conclusion: SynthGait-19k数据集和GaitXFormer模型为步态参数估计提供了新的重要资源，展示了合成数据训练的可行性和有效性，同时揭示了不同步态参数对域迁移的敏感度差异以及HMR与下游任务之间的非单调关系。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of clinically meaningful gait parameters from monocular video is important for scalable mobility assessment, yet progress is limited by the small scale, restricted viewpoints, and limited visual diversity of existing datasets. We introduce SynthGait-19k, a physically grounded synthetic video dataset containing 19,272 walking videos derived from 6,427 MoCap sequences across 437 subjects, with paired SMPL motion and annotations for six gait parameters. To construct the dataset, we develop Gait2Vid, which unifies heterogeneous MoCap recordings through SMPL and synthesizes diverse RGB walking videos under controllable viewpoints and scene appearances. We assess the generated videos for consistency with their conditioning gait kinematics and validate extracted gait events against force-platform measurements. Using SynthGait-19K, we benchmark direct RGB, pose-based, biomechanical, and human-mesh-recovery approaches and analyze viewpoint, training-data scale, and synthetic-to-real domain shift. We also introduce GaitXFormer as a direct RGB reference model for estimating gait parameters. Synthetic supervision transfers effectively to real videos across both GaitXFormer and a pose-based architecture, demonstrating utility across different representations. We further find that spatial gait parameters are more sensitive to visual domain shift and that improved HMR reconstruction alone does not necessarily translate to improved downstream gait estimation.

</details>


### [224] [DriveMotion: A Large-Scale Multi-Source Benchmark for Driver Motion Sequence Modeling and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08117)
*Yuhang Wang,Chuheng Wei,Jingxin Yang,Xishun Liao,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveMotion是一个多源驾驶员连续运动预测基准，包含393小时、360名驾驶员的133关键点运动序列，通过动力学锚定评估和流形增强训练，显著提升驾驶员运动预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶员数据集多关注短视频片段中的预定义行为识别，而人体运动预测基准则主要针对车外场景。驾驶员运动可提供行为、注意力与近期驾驶意图的重要线索，但缺乏专门用于连续驾驶员运动预测的基准。

Method: 构建DriveMotion多源基准，整合自然驾驶数据、公开车内视频及AIDE数据集，统一为含关节有效性掩码与驾驶上下文的133关键点表示。采用动力学锚定评估策略，基于CAN信号离线识别的车辆机动来放置预测窗口，且推理时不向模型提供CAN。训练采用流形增强策略，结合多源语料训练。

Result: 预机动窗口中的手臂运动比路线匹配的稳定驾驶对照组高3.4倍；锚定窗口上学习模型相比持续性预测降低误差最高15%；流形增强训练使预测来源的部件状态F1较零运动参考提高44%；多源训练相比仅BATON训练使留出网页司机预测误差降低38%。

Conclusion: DriveMotion提供了身份分离的数据划分、固定评估子集和参考实现，支持可复现的连续驾驶员运动预测评估，为驾驶相关人体运动预测研究提供标准化基准。

Abstract: Driver motion can provide cues to ongoing behavior, attention, and near-term driving intent. However, most existing driver-centric datasets focus on recognizing predefined driver behaviors from short video clips, while human motion forecasting benchmarks largely target motion outside the vehicle. We introduce DriveMotion, a multi-source benchmark for continuous driver motion forecasting. DriveMotion contains 393 hours of 133-keypoint motion sequences at 10 Hz from 360 drivers, integrating naturalistic driving data, curated public in-cabin videos, and the AIDE dataset into a unified representation with per-joint validity masks and synchronized driving context. Naturalistic driving contains long periods of limited body movement, making uniformly sampled evaluation dominated by persistence and less sensitive to brief but behaviorally meaningful motion. To address this, we use dynamics-anchored evaluation, placing forecasting windows around vehicle maneuvers identified offline from CAN signals without providing CAN to the model at inference. Arm motion in pre-maneuver windows is 3.4x greater than in route-matched stable-driving controls. On these anchored windows, learned models reduce forecasting error over persistence by up to 15%, while maneuver-enriched training improves forecast-derived Part-State F1 by 44% over the zero-motion reference. Training on the full multi-source corpus further reduces forecasting error on held-out web drivers by 38% compared with BATON-only training. DriveMotion provides identity-disjoint splits, fixed evaluation subsets, and reference implementations for reproducible evaluation of continuous driver motion forecasting. The dataset and benchmark are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/HenryYHW/DriveMotion

</details>


### [225] [Hyperspectral Anomaly Detection via Group Sparse Low-Rank Tensor Factorization With Automatic Anomaly Grouping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08121)
*Quan Yu,Yu-Hong Dai,Xiongjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于组稀疏低秩张量分解与自动异常分组的高光谱异常检测方法（GSAA），通过低tubal秩背景建模和潜在分组图实现自适应异常分组，并扩展谱-空融合版本GSAA-SS，在五个真实数据集上验证了优越的检测性能和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩张量高光谱异常检测方法存在两方面局限：一是计算成本高，直接使用张量秩正则化导致效率低下；二是灵活性不足，难以有效刻画具有空间结构性的异常目标。

Method: 1) 在张量因子上施加组稀疏约束以刻画低tubal秩背景，避免直接张量秩正则化的高计算成本；2) 引入潜在分组图构建自动异常分组惩罚项，使异常组能够从数据中自适应推断而非预先在像素级定义；3) 在光谱域和空间域分别应用GSAA并将检测图融合，形成谱-空版本GSAA-SS；4) 开发具有收敛性保证的线性化交替方向乘子法（LADMM）进行求解。

Result: 在五个真实高光谱数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在检测性能上优于多种现有先进方法，同时具有有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 提出的GSAA方法有效解决了现有低秩张量异常检测方法计算成本高和灵活性不足的问题，通过自动异常分组和谱-空信息融合实现了更优的检测效果，并具备可靠的收敛保证。

Abstract: Low-rank tensor modeling has become an effective tool for hyperspectral anomaly detection. However, existing methods still suffer from high computational cost and limited flexibility in characterizing spatially structured anomalies. To address these issues, this paper proposes a hyperspectral anomaly detection method based on group sparse low-rank tensor factorization with automatic anomaly grouping (GSAA). Specifically, the low tubal rank background is characterized by imposing group sparsity on tensor factors, which provides an efficient alternative to direct tensor rank regularization. For anomaly modeling, a latent grouping map is introduced to build an automatic anomaly grouping penalty, allowing anomaly groups to be adaptively inferred from the data rather than predefined at the pixel level. To further exploit complementary spectral and spatial information, GSAA is applied in both domains, and the resulting detection maps are fused to form a spectral--spatial version of GSAA, termed GSAA-SS. An efficient linearized alternating direction method of multipliers algorithm with convergence guarantee is developed to solve the resulting model. Experimental results on five real hyperspectral datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior detection performance and competitive computational efficiency compared with several state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [226] [Observe Before You Alert: Adaptive Driver Alerting with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08130)
*Yuhang Wang,Lingyao Li,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLAlert是一个视觉语言警报框架，将驾驶警报生成建模为SILENT、OBSERVE、ALERT三动作策略，利用Qwen3-VL-4B作为安全证据生成器，通过结构化信念跨度的隐藏状态池化实现自适应警报决策。在VLAlert-Bench基准上取得最优性能，并在迁移测试中显著提升警报指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事故预期模型仅输出二元风险分数，需要阈值化处理模糊场景，缺乏在证据不足时决定"何时发出警报"的序贯决策能力。这是一个在部分可观测环境下的时序决策问题，现有方法未能充分建模。

Method: VLAlert将警报生成视为三动作策略：SILENT（静默）、OBSERVE（观察）、ALERT（警报）。其中OBSERVE作为内部证据收集决策，可延迟不确定警报并改变下一观察窗口，形成轻量感知-行动循环。使用Qwen3-VL-4B作为安全证据生成器，从结构化信念跨度池化隐藏状态以形成紧凑的危险估计和策略预测表示。

Result: 在VLAlert-Bench验证集上，VLAlert取得最高部署效用，DAUS为0.4878（对比Open-BADAS的0.4752）；AUROC、AP_tick、F1_t和平衡准确率分别从0.610/0.176/0.276/0.581提升至0.689/0.195/0.297/0.648。在221个ADAS-TO-Critic片段上，R@5s从74.2%提升至88.7%，F1从0.585提升至0.686。

Conclusion: 自适应观察机制与安全导向的VLM表示能够为面向驾驶员的警报决策带来可衡量的性能提升，验证了三动作决策框架在驾驶警报任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Driver alerting from dashcam video requires sequential decision-making under partial observability: a system must decide not only whether a scene is risky, but also when the evidence is sufficient to warn. Most existing accident anticipation models output a binary risk score, leaving ambiguous scenes to be handled by thresholding. We propose VLAlert, a vision-language alerting framework that casts warning generation as a tri-action policy over SILENT, OBSERVE, and ALERT. The OBSERVE action acts as an internal evidence-gathering decision that delays uncertain warnings and changes the next observation window, creating a lightweight perception-action loop for adaptive alerting. VLAlert uses Qwen3-VL-4B as a safety-evidence generator and pools hidden states from structured belief spans to form compact representations for danger estimation and policy prediction. We evaluate VLAlert on VLAlert-Bench, a unified per-tick benchmark from four real-world dashcam alert datasets, and further test transfer to held-out naturalistic ADAS takeover clips. On VLAlert-Bench validation, VLAlert achieves the highest deployment-oriented utility among tested baselines, with DAUS 0.4878 compared with 0.4752 for Open-BADAS, and improves AUROC, AP_tick, F1_t, and balanced accuracy from 0.610, 0.176, 0.276, and 0.581 to 0.689, 0.195, 0.297, and 0.648, respectively. On 221 held-out ADAS-TO-Critic clips, VLAlert improves R@5s from 74.2% to 88.7% and F1 from 0.585 to 0.686. These results indicate that adaptive observation and safety-focused VLM representations provide measurable gains for driver-facing alert decisions.

</details>


### [227] [MRI-Guided Reslice-Refined Cross-Slice SDF Reconstruction of the Left Ventricle from Cardiac MRI with Sparse Axial Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08148)
*Quanxin Zheng,Shuai Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MR-RS-SDFR框架，一种逐案例隐式符号距离场(SDF)方法，用于从心脏磁共振(CMR)数据和稀疏轴向弱掩膜中重建连续的三维左心室(LV)心内膜表面。该方法通过跨切片SDF初始化和两种互补信号（MRI边缘场法向对齐与可微重切片Dice/轮廓一致性）进行细化，在多种弱掩膜生成器和稀疏度设置下均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从CMR数据重建三维左心室心内膜表面极具挑战性，尤其是在仅有少量轴向切片标注时：穿平面几何约束不足，且自动生成的二维掩膜可能将分割误差传播到重建形状中。

Method: MR-RS-SDFR方法包括：(1) 从轴向和纵向几何线索构建跨切片SDF初始化；(2) 利用两个互补信号细化场：MRI边缘场法向对齐（提供不依赖弱掩膜的图像派生边界线索）和可微重切片Dice与轮廓一致性（保持与观测平面的一致性）。

Result: 在稀疏16层设置下，使用Medical SAM3掩膜的最终重建达到0.928 Dice和3.80mm HD95。不同稀疏度（4至64层）及三种弱掩膜生成器（LOO TransUNet、LOO nnU-Net、Medical SAM3）下均优于完整GHD+DVS方法。最终Dice从稀疏4到16显著提升后在稀疏64处饱和。

Conclusion: MRI引导的逐案例SDF细化是一种有效的重建策略，能跨越不同弱掩膜生成器和监督密度保持稳健性能，且不同上游生成器虽错误分布不同，但最终重建精度可能趋同。

Abstract: Reconstructing a three-dimensional left-ventricular (LV) endocardial surface from cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) data is challenging when supervision is available on only a small number of axial slices. Through-plane geometry is weakly constrained, and automatically generated two-dimensional masks can propagate segmentation errors into the recovered shape. We present MR-RS-SDFR, a per-case implicit signed distance field (SDF) framework that reconstructs a continuous LV surface from a CMR volume and sparse axial weak masks. The method first builds a cross-slice SDF initialization from axial and longitudinal geometric cues and then refines the field using two complementary signals: MRI edge-field normal alignment, which provides an image-derived boundary cue independent of the weak masks, and differentiable reslice Dice and contour consistency, which preserve agreement with the observed planes. We evaluate three weak-mask generators -- LOO TransUNet, LOO nnU-Net, and an off-the-shelf Medical SAM3 model used without MM-WHS-specific training or fine-tuning -- and five sparsity levels from 4 to 64 axial planes. In the sparse-16 setting, final MR-RS-SDFR reconstruction reaches 0.928 Dice and 3.80mm HD95 with Medical SAM3 masks. The upstream generators do not exhibit a single common ranking across 2D and dense 3D segmentation, and nnU-Net- and Medical-SAM3-driven sparse reconstruction achieve the same mean final Dice despite different upstream error profiles. Across all three sparse-16 mask sources, MR-RS-SDFR is numerically better than protocol-matched full GHD+DVS in both Dice and HD95. Final Dice improves markedly from sparse-4 to sparse-16 and then saturates at the reported precision through sparse-64. These results support MRI-guided per-case SDF refinement as a reconstruction strategy that remains effective across weak-mask generators and supervision densities.

</details>


### [228] [SciFigure2Code: An AI-Reconstructed Benchmark for Scientific Figure-to-Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08155)
*Wentao Li,Yibo Wu,Yizhe Chen,Ruixuan Chen,Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Zhao Leyi,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SciFigure2Code，一个AI重建基准，将已发表科学图表还原为可编辑的Python展示程序，而非恢复原始数据或源码。通过角色分工的Codex代理生成、执行、视觉精修和审计"银标准"展示程序，构建了含6,740个已审阅面板及337个平衡测试集（SciFigureBench）的资源。评估14个零样本模型发现执行、多组件布局、坐标轴、图例和科学标签仍是薄弱环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图表是研究声明被检查和复用的界面，但最终发表的图板很少公开产生它们的数据或绘图代码。从像素中恢复这种隐藏来源是欠定问题，因此需要一个新的评估方向——展示恢复（presentation recovery），即生成保留科学面板排列和阅读方式的可编辑程序。

Method: 提出"重建与审计"协议：角色专门化的Codex代理生成、执行、视觉精修并审计"银标准"展示程序，捕捉几何、视觉层次、编码、注释和排版，不声称恢复原始测量或作者源代码。将已发表面板转化为可审计的参考包，构建含6,740个已审阅面板的数据集及SciFigureBench（337个面板，覆盖31个图表子类型、5个领域、3个复杂度层级）。

Result: 评估14个零-shot模型（仅图像及带标题两种设置），发现执行能力、多组件布局、坐标轴、图例和科学标签仍是薄弱点。Claude Opus 4.7在仅图像模式取得最高Overall分数，Claude Opus 4.6在带标题重建中领先，两阶段"先计划后编码"提示在全部4个测试模型上均提升Overall。

Conclusion: SciFigure2Code提供了一个可审计的测试平台，用于评估能够构建可编辑、视觉保真的科学图表展示的智能体，推动科学可视化复原与可复现性研究。

Abstract: Scientific figures are the interface through which research claims are inspected and reused, but final published panels rarely expose the data or plotting code that produced them. Recovering this hidden provenance from pixels is therefore underdetermined. We introduce SciFigure2Code, an AI-reconstructed benchmark that instead evaluates presentation recovery: generating editable Python programs that preserve how a scientific panel is arranged and read. Role-specialized Codex agents generate, execute, visually refine, and audit silver-standard presentation programs that capture geometry, visual hierarchy, encodings, annotations, and typography without claiming to recover original measurements or author source code. This reconstruction-and-audit protocol turns final published panels into auditable reference packages; the resulting resource contains 6,740 reviewed panels and SciFigureBench, a balanced 337-panel test set across 31 chart subtypes, five domains, and three complexity levels. Across 14 zero-shot models in image-only and caption-assisted settings, execution, multi-component layouts, axes, legends, and scientific labels remain weak. Claude Opus 4.7 achieves the highest image-only Overall score, Claude Opus 4.6 leads caption-assisted reconstruction, and two-stage plan-then-code prompting improves Overall for all four tested models. SciFigure2Code provides an auditable testbed for agents that construct editable, visually faithful scientific figure presentations.

</details>


### [229] [Boundary Voting Network for Ambiguity-Aware Timestamp-Supervised Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08167)
*Runzhong Zhang,Yueqi Duan,Yang Chen,Weipeng Hu,Chen Cai,Suchen Wang,Yap-Peng Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出边界投票网络（Boundary Voting Network），解决时间戳监督动作分割中边界定位不确定性的问题。通过将视频级全局先验知识分层传播到动作过渡区域，生成关键动作表示作为投票来增强过渡区域特征、优化边界定位，在GTEA、50Salads和Breakfast数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间戳监督动作分割中，动作边界精确定位至关重要（用于生成帧级伪标签）。然而现有方法在动作过渡区域因特征判别性不足而存在边界定位的内在不确定性，导致生成的伪标签在模糊区域不稳定不可靠，最终降低分割模型性能。

Method: 提出边界投票网络，通过层次化地将视频级全局先验知识传播到局部动作过渡区域来缓解特征模糊性。生成关键动作表示作为全视频范围内的投票，并针对动作过渡区域，使所有投票协同贡献于动作过渡特征增强和边界定位细化。

Result: 在GTEA、50Salads和Breakfast三个数据集上的大量实验证明了该方法在时间戳监督动作分割任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过全局投票机制增强过渡区域特征与优化边界定位，能够有效提升时间戳监督下动作分割的性能和伪标签的可靠性。

Abstract: Timestamp-supervised action segmentation aims to segment and classify actions in untrimmed videos with a random frame annotated per action. Precisely localizing action boundaries from timestamp annotations is crucial for this setting, as it enables generating framewise pseudo-labels and applying the well-explored fully-supervised training. However, prevailing methods struggle with intrinsic uncertainty in boundary localization due to less discriminative features in action-transiting regions. This imprecise boundary estimation significantly reduces the stability and reliability of the generated pseudo-labels in ambiguous action-transiting regions, consequently resulting in performance deterioration of the trained segmentation models. In our paper, we introduce the boundary voting network that mitigates feature ambiguity by hierarchically propagating video-level global prior knowledge into local action-transiting regions. By generating key action representations as votes throughout the video and targeting action-transiting regions, all votes collaboratively contribute to action-transiting feature enhancement and boundary localization refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on GTEA, 50Salads, and Breakfast datasets.

</details>


### [230] [WSPolypNet: Weakly Supervised Polyp Localization in Colonoscopy Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08182)
*Giseong Hwang,Minjae Jo,Yeonghyeon Park,Kyeonghun Kim,Seoyeon Han,Donghoon Han,Haneul Kim,Yului Jeong,Insung Hwang,Pa Hong,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WSPolypNet，一种仅使用视频级标签的弱监督息肉定位框架。它利用3D CNN生成类激活图(CAMs)，通过多视角策略增强定位线索，并将其作为点提示输入MedSAM2以细化分割掩膜。实验结果表明在多个IoU阈值下定位性能显著提升，小息肉的CorLoc@0.5从16.01%提升至30.97%，召回率达94.51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜检查视频的密集帧级标注成本过高，目前缺乏仅依赖视频级弱监督标签就能实现准确息肉定位的有效方法，因此需要一种能大幅降低空间标注需求的弱监督框架。

Method: 提出WSPolypNet框架，包含两个核心阶段：(1) 使用3D CNN在视频级监督下训练，生成类激活图(CAMs)以识别候选息肉区域，无需帧级空间标注；(2) 利用多视角策略增强CAM派生的定位线索，并将其作为点提示输入MedSAM2，由MedSAM2跨视频传播分割掩膜，根据息肉边界细化粗定位线索。

Result: 在IoU阈值0.3、0.5和0.7下，CorLoc分数分别为47.80%、43.68%和35.01%，而单视角设置下仅为36.87%、33.72%和27.94%。对于小息肉，多视角策略将CorLoc@0.5从16.01%提升至30.97%。框架整体召回率达到94.51%。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了弱监督时空学习在显著减少空间标注需求的前提下进行息肉定位的巨大潜力，为结肠镜视频中息肉定位提供了一条低成本、高效益的技术路线。

Abstract: Because dense frame-level annotation of colonoscopy videos is costly, we propose WSPolypNet, a weakly supervised framework for polyp localization using only video-level labels. WSPolypNet employs a 3D convolutional neural network trained with video-level supervision to generate class activation maps (CAMs), which identify candidate polyp regions without requiring frame-level spatial annotations. The CAM-derived localization cues are further enhanced using a multi-view strategy and provided to MedSAM2 as point prompts. MedSAM2 then propagates segmentation masks across the video, refining the coarse localization cues according to polyp boundaries. WSPolypNet achieved CorLoc scores of 47.80%, 43.68%, and 35.01% at IoU thresholds of 0.3, 0.5, and 0.7, respectively, compared with 36.87%, 33.72%, and 27.94% in the single-view setting. For small polyps, the multi-view strategy improved CorLoc@0.5 from 16.01% to 30.97%. The framework also achieved a recall of 94.51%. These results demonstrate the potential of weakly supervised spatiotemporal learning to substantially reduce spatial annotation requirements for polyp localization in colonoscopy videos.

</details>


### [231] [PhysFlow: Physics-Aware Optical Flow for Motion Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08215)
*Cong Wang,Hanxin Zhu,Yonglin Tian,Jiayi Luo,Ruiqi Song,Boyi Sun,Long Chen,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysFlow，一个两阶段框架，通过将视频生成分解为"物理感知光流生成"和"光流引导的外观合成"来提升生成视频的物理合理性。框架包含PA-Flow（物理感知光流生成器）和FlowRender（光流引导视频生成器），并构建了含50K视频序列的物理数据集PhysVideo用于显式物理监督训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型能够生成视觉上引人注目的视频，但确保物理一致性和合理的动态仍然是一个根本性挑战。作者观察到物理规律主要编码在运动模式中，因此希望通过显式建模运动来提升视频的物理合理性。

Method: PhysFlow采用两阶段框架：第一阶段，PA-Flow利用物理感知注意力模块分别建模运动属性和材料属性对全局运动与局部形变的影响，生成光流视频作为运动的显式表示；第二阶段，FlowRender利用解耦的运动表示作为引导，合成真实纹理和外观，生成最终视频。同时构建了PhysVideo物理数据集用于显式物理监督。

Result: 大量实验表明，PhysFlow相比现有方法能生成具有更优物理合理性且保持高视觉保真度的视频。

Conclusion: 通过将视频生成分解为运动生成与外观合成两个解耦阶段，并引入物理感知的显式运动表示（光流）和物理数据集监督，PhysFlow有效地提升了生成视频的物理合理性。

Abstract: Video generation models have recently attracted substantial attention for their ability to generate visually compelling videos, yet ensuring physically consistent and plausible dynamics still remains a fundamental challenge, driving a growing line of research on physical realism in video generation. To address this challenge, motivated by the fact that physical regularities are primarily encoded in motion patterns, we propose PhysFlow, a novel two-stage framework for improving the physical plausibility of generated videos by decomposing video generation into motion-aware optical flow generation followed by motion-conditioned appearance synthesis. Specifically, PhysFlow consists of a physics-aware optical-flow video generator called PA-Flow and a flow-guided video generator called FlowRender. During the first stage, PA-Flow employs a physics-aware attention module to model how motion attributes and material properties influence global motion and local deformation, respectively, and generates an optical flow video as an explicit representation of motion. In the second stage, FlowRender leverages the decoupled motion representation as guidance to synthesize realistic textures and appearances, ultimately producing the final physically plausible video. To further support model training with explicit physical supervision, we construct PhysVideo, a physics-based video dataset generated with a physics engine and 3D-GS rendering, containing 10K foreground objects and 50K realistic video sequences with annotations of motion and material properties. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed PhysFlow generates videos with superior physical plausibility while maintaining high visual fidelity compared with existing methods.

</details>


### [232] [VeriScene: Reconstructing Crime Scenes from Legal Evidence via World-Model Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08342)
*Kevin Chuanpu Fu,Yongsen Zheng,Zee Kin Yeong,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了VeriScene，一个编排世界模型的智能体，用于从法医照片和目击者陈述中重建犯罪现场并重演犯罪过程。它在审计循环中将多模态证据融合为可引用的叙述，通过试探性回放和约束注入验证物理合理性，最终生成犯罪重演视频。在25个犯罪场景基准上达到0.9014证据覆盖率和0.7217事实一致性，比端到端多模态LLM基线在事实一致性上提升20.35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在法医领域的犯罪现场重建应用中存在关键局限：直接输入原始杂乱证据会导致证据被静默丢弃、矛盾证词被忽视、运动生成违反证据记录，无法满足法医场景对可追溯性和物理合理性的严格要求。

Method: VeriScene作为编排世界模型的智能体，包含三个核心环节：(1)在审计循环中迭代地将证据融合为带引用的叙述；(2)通过世界模型中的试探性回放验证假设动力学，并注入纠正性约束；(3)从融合的关键帧渲染犯罪重演视频。整个过程确保每条声明可追溯到证据且每个动作物理上合理。

Result: 在包含25个犯罪场景、139张法医风格照片和65份带有故意不可靠性的陈述的基准上，VeriScene在20个测试场景中达到0.9014证据覆盖率和0.7217事实一致性，在事实一致性上比端到端多模态LLM基线高出20.35%，时间一致性高出34.88%，且可在四个LLM编排后端上泛化，每场景成本仅1.82美元。

Conclusion: 基于证据驱动的智能体编排方法优于直接的端到端方法，能够有效解决世界模型在法医重建中的可信度和物理合理性挑战，为多模态证据融合的犯罪现场重建提供了可靠且经济可行的解决方案。

Abstract: World models take multimodal inputs like text, photos, and diagrams to generate dynamic scenes in accordance with the laws of physics, thus opening a compelling application: fusing multimodal legal evidence to re-create a crime scene and re-enact how an offence could have been committed. However, feeding the raw, unorganized evidence into a world model fails in forensic use: it silently drops evidence, glosses over contradictory testimony, and produces motion that violates the evidentiary record. This paper presents VeriScene, an agent that orchestrates the world model: it reconstructs crime scenes from forensic photographs and witness statements of varying reliability, keeping every claim traceable to evidence and every motion physically plausible. VeriScene iteratively fuses the evidence into a cited narrative under an auditing loop, verifies the hypothesized dynamics via probe rollouts in the world model with corrective constraint injection, and renders the offence as a re-enactment video from a fused keyframe. On a benchmark of 25 crime scenarios across 7 physically-driven case types (139 forensic-style photographs and 65 statements with planted unreliability), VeriScene attains 0.9014 evidence coverage and 0.7217 factual consistency (0-1 scale) on the 20 test scenes, outperforming an end-to-end multimodal-LLM baseline by 20.35% in factual consistency and 34.88% in temporal coherence, while generalizing across four LLM orchestration backends at USD 1.82 per scene.

</details>


### [233] [CoVeR: Coverage-Based Token Pruning for Multi-View 3D Reasoning in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08345)
*Nhat-Tan Bui,Varshini Elangovan,Arun Reddy Anugu,Sreyas Mohan,Wei Ye,Dilin Wang,JQ Huang,Rakesh Ranjan,Aviral Chharia,Fernando De la Torre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoVeR是一种确定性、免训练的多视角图像视觉标记选择器，仅利用标记坐标实现空间全覆盖，在严格预算下解决3D推理中现有标记剪枝方法的冗余和覆盖率不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将3D场景表示为多视角图像可用2D VLM进行3D推理，但会产生数千个冗余视觉标记且成本随视角增长。现有两类标记剪枝方法（学习型重要性排序和体素化）在3D多视角设定下各有局限：前者保留近重复标记导致场景大部分未被表示，后者无法强制精确预算且在视角重叠时饱和。作者发现空间覆盖与3D推理表现相关，因此需要一种能同时解决两者局限的方法。

Method: 提出CoVeR，一个确定性、免训练的标记选择器，仅使用标记坐标（不依赖任何学习信号）。其核心思想是选择能共同覆盖场景每个区域的标记集合，从而同时实现：强制每个场景的精确预算、打破体素化的饱和瓶颈、避免学习型重要性方法的近重复选择。

Result: 在三个3D推理基准上均优于先前SOTA方法，并作为即插即用模块在四种VLM上泛化验证。仅用约8%的视觉标记即可保留93.5%的全标记性能，在基准上平均超过SOTA 3.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 空间覆盖与3D推理性能相关；CoVeR通过纯坐标驱动的确定性选择方法，在不依赖学习信号的情况下有效平衡了标记预算与空间覆盖，成为3D多视角场景下通用且高效的视觉标记剪枝方案。

Abstract: Representing a 3D scene as multi-view images allows 2D VLMs to reason in 3D by reusing priors from pre-training, sidestepping the scarcity of annotated 3D data. However, it produces thousands of redundant visual tokens whose cost grows with every view. Existing visual token pruners fall into two families, each limited in the 3D multi-view setting. Learned importance methods rank tokens by attention or encoder features; because redundancy here is fundamentally spatial, they keep near-duplicate tokens from a few prominent regions and leave most of the scene unrepresented. Voxelization methods improve spatial coverage but cannot enforce an exact token budget and saturate as multi-view observations overlap in 3D, capping retention well below the target. We show that spatial coverage is associated with 3D reasoning performance and introduce CoVeR, a deterministic, training-free selector that uses only token coordinates, with no learned signals. CoVeR selects tokens that collectively cover every region of the scene, and solves the limitations of both families: it enforces an exact per-scene budget, breaks the voxelization saturation plateau, and avoids the near-duplicate selections of learned importance. Extensive experiments show CoVeR outperforms prior SOTAs on all three 3D reasoning benchmarks and generalizes as a plug-and-play module tested across four VLMs. Notably, with only $\approx$8% of visual tokens, it preserves 93.5% of full-token performance, surpassing SOTA by 3.9 percentage points on average across benchmarks.

</details>


### [234] [Segment Any Motion with Radar: Robust Multimodal Moving-Object Segmentation and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08346)
*Jue Wang,Xuan Wang,Hao Zhou,Ruixiang Zhou,Yixuan Zhou,Tianshuo Yuan,Jieming Ma,Jie Zhang,Fei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RGBTR-Motion基准数据集和SAM-Radar框架。RGBTR-Motion是首个同步提供RGB、热成像、雷达流及稠密实例掩码和时间一致性身份标注的固定摄像头监控基准。SAM-Radar构建于SAM 3之上，通过雷达感知检测器融合RGBT特征与雷达回波，并利用运动监督训练检测器拒绝杂波；跟踪器将雷达回波作为物理证据，可跨越低可见性或遮挡期维持目标身份。实验显示IoU达0.7027，MOTA/HOTA/IDF1分别提升0.2977/0.1603/0.2857。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动目标感知需判别真实运动区域并保持实例身份一致性。基于外观、光流或轨迹估计的方法在光照差、恶劣天气、反射和遮挡下会丢失证据。雷达可直接测量径向速度，是天然解决方案，但现有基准未同时提供雷达测量、稠密运动实例掩码和时间一致性身份标注。

Method: 1) 构建RGBTR-Motion：同步校准的固定摄像头基准，含RGB、热成像、雷达流及稠密实例掩码和时间一致身份。2) SAM-Radar框架：基于SAM 3，雷达感知检测器融合校准的RGBT特征与投影到图像位置的雷达回波，通过运动监督（投影回波的前景分类）实现无文本提示的杂波拒绝；跟踪器将接受的雷达回波与个体轨迹关联，作为视觉退化目标仍存在的物理证据，可桥接短暂低可见或遮挡期并重连身份。

Result: SAM-Radar在分割上达到0.7027 IoU和0.8090 F1-50；在跟踪指标上，MOTA、HOTA和IDF1分别比最强竞争方法提升0.2977、0.1603和0.2857。

Conclusion: 融合雷达径向速度证据可显著增强视觉退化场景下的运动目标分割与身份保持能力，SAM-Radar验证了跨模态融合（RGB+热成像+雷达）在监控场景中优于单模态或双模态方法。

Abstract: Moving-object perception must decide which image regions correspond to real motion and keep every instance identified over time. Methods that read motion from appearance, optical flow, or estimated trajectories lose that evidence under poor illumination, adverse weather, reflections, and occlusion. Radar is a natural remedy because it measures radial velocity directly instead of inferring it from photometric correspondence. However, existing benchmarks do not jointly provide radar measurements, dense moving-instance masks, and temporally consistent identities for surveillance. We therefore introduce RGBTR-Motion, a synchronized and calibrated fixed-camera benchmark that pairs RGB, thermal, and radar streams with dense instance masks and temporally consistent identities across diverse surveillance scenes. We also develop SAM-Radar, an RGB, thermal, and radar-based segmentation and tracking framework built on SAM 3. SAM-Radar's radar-aware detector fuses calibrated RGBT features with radar returns that are grounded at their projected image locations, and motion supervision, implemented as foreground classification of those projected returns, teaches the detector to reject clutter without any text prompt. The tracker associates accepted radar returns with individual trajectories and uses them as physical evidence that a visually degraded target remains present. This allows it to bridge short periods of low visibility or occlusion and reconnect a reappearing target to its existing identity instead of starting a new track. SAM-Radar attains 0.7027 IoU and 0.8090 F1-50, and raises MOTA, HOTA, and IDF1 by 0.2977, 0.1603, and 0.2857 over the strongest competing values.

</details>


### [235] [ReMoMask-2: Latent Retrieval-Augmented Masked Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08365)
*Yiran Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Ling Shao,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReMoMask和ReMoMask-2两个检索增强文本到运动生成模型。ReMoMask通过层级双向动量对比学习、语义时空注意力、拓扑结构化掩码三种机制，解决粗粒度检索与融合问题；ReMoMask-2则直接在生成器的预量化潜空间中重建检索数据库，并用轻量投影器对齐文本查询，消除表示间隙。在HumanML3D、KIT-ML和SnapMoGen上取得SOTA检索精度，其中ReMoMask-2在KIT-ML和SnapMoGen上获得最低FID，且单阶段推理速度最快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强文本到运动生成模型存在两大挑战：1）粗粒度的检索与融合机制忽略了人体运动的层级、时空拓扑结构；2）检索到的证据存放在与生成器潜空间分离的语义空间中，存在表示间隙，导致检索信息无法被生成器直接有效利用。

Method: 提出两个模型：ReMoMask采用结构感知的RAG框架，包含三种机制——层级双向动量对比学习（对齐全局与部件级特征）、语义时空注意力（拓扑感知融合）、拓扑结构化掩码（自适应掩码强制部件级接地）。ReMoMask-2在生成器的预量化潜空间中重建检索数据库，并通过蒸馏轻量投影器对齐文本查询，使生成器能直接消费检索运动的语义内容。

Result: 在HumanML3D、KIT-ML和SnapMoGen三个数据集上的实验表明：检索器达到SOTA精度；ReMoMask-2在KIT-ML和SnapMoGen上取得最低FID；其单掩码变换器阶段超越了ReMoMask完整两阶段流水线，并实现最快推理速度。

Conclusion: 本文验证了结构感知的检索与融合以及统一潜空间检索表征对文本到运动生成的有效性，ReMoMask-2的简化单阶段架构在性能与效率上均优于更复杂的双阶段方案，为RAG-T2M领域提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Text-to-motion (T2M) generation maps natural language to human joint movements, aiding gaming, VR, and robotics. Retrieval-Augmented Text-to-Motion (RAG-T2M) improves generation on complex descriptions by conditioning on retrieved motion-text pairs. However, existing RAG-T2M models face two challenges: coarse-grained retrieval and fusion mechanisms overlook the hierarchical, spatial-temporal topology of human motion, and a representation gap exists because retrieved evidence resides in a semantic space separate from the generator's latents. To address the first, we present ReMoMask, a structure-aware RAG framework coupling Hierarchical Bidirectional Momentum (HBM) contrastive learning to align global and part-level features with text; Semantic Spatial-Temporal Attention (SSTA) for topology-aware fusion; and Topology Structured Masking (TSM) to force robust part-level grounding via adaptive masking. To address the second, we introduce ReMoMask-2, which rebuilds the retrieval database directly within the generator's pre-quantization latent space and aligns text queries via a distilled lightweight projector, allowing the generator to directly consume the retrieved motion's semantic content. Extensive experiments on HumanML3D, KIT-ML, and SnapMoGen demonstrate our retriever achieves state-of-the-art accuracy, while ReMoMask-2 attains the lowest FID on KIT-ML and SnapMoGen; notably, its single mask-transformer stage surpasses ReMoMask's full two-stage pipeline and delivers the fastest inference.

</details>


### [236] [To Adapt or Not to Adapt? Selective Adaptation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08367)
*Siru Jiang,Yuwei Liang,Jian Liang,Ran He,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性适应(Selective Adaptation)的新问题：通过交叉增强相似度(CAS)判断测试样本是否需要进行测试时适应(TTA)，跳过无益或有害的适应操作，在跳过约85%适应过程的情况下仍能保持甚至提升模型准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时适应(TTA)方法存在两种失败模式：适应经常是无效的（不改变模型预测），甚至是有害的（将原本正确的预测翻转为错误）。因此作者提出疑问：能否识别并跳过这些无益或有害的适应操作。

Method: 提出选择性适应(Selective Adaptation)新问题，并引入交叉增强相似度(CAS)作为简单基线——仅当测试样本在不同增强视图下的预测相似度较低时才执行适应，否则跳过适应过程。

Result: CAS不仅保持了整体准确率，在某些情况下甚至提升了准确率，即使跳过了将近85%的适应过程。

Conclusion: 选择性适应是一个值得探索的新方向，CAS作为简单基线展示了其有效性，作者希望其他研究者能在此基础上进一步超越该基线的性能。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) has emerged as a prominent strategy for adapting vision-language models to distribution shifts during inference. We conduct a per-sample analysis of model predictions before and after adaptation, and observe two failure modes in existing TTA methods that echo previous work. Adaptations are frequently negligible, yielding no change in the model's predictions, and more severely, they can be detrimental by flipping previously correct predictions to incorrect ones. This naturally raises a question: Can we identify and skip such negligible or harmful adaptations? In this work, we introduce a new problem of selective adaptation, which aims to determine whether a given test sample should undergo adaptation or be skipped. To this end, we propose Cross-Augmentation Similarity (CAS), a simple baseline that performs adaptation only when predictions across augmented views exhibit low similarity.
