Notably, CAS not only preserves but in some cases improves overall accuracy, even when skipping nearly 85% of the adaptation process. We hope other researchers will explore this new direction and surpass the performance of our baseline. Our code is available at https://github.com/sirujiang/selective-adaptation.

</details>


### [237] [GALoc: Gravity Aligned Wireframes for Depth-Free Monocular Floorplan Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08385)
*Jeahn Han,Minji Kim,Jeongbin Sohn,Jonghyeok Park,Matthias Wuest,Pyojin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GALoc 提出一种几何优先的室内定位框架，用重力对齐的线框替代深度预测，通过约束矩阵和 SE(2) 搜索实现无深度网络的楼层平面匹配定位，在多种场景下优于或持平基于深度的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内定位方法依赖深度网络，在杂乱场景中鲁棒性差；而楼层平面图本身是紧凑且外观不变的定位地图，但缺乏不依赖深度的有效匹配方法。

Method: 采用几何优先框架：利用单目RGB、相机内参、相对位姿和IMU方向构建线性约束矩阵（编码垂直性和共面性），通过全局搜索求使最小奇异值最小的相机标定；再将矫正后的线框经闭式、FOV一致的变换投影为鸟瞰图布局，与楼层平面图进行无度量SE(2)搜索匹配。

Result: 在Structured3D端到端评估、Gibson带标定噪声评估及真实自采序列上，当足够墙体几何可见时，GALoc匹配或超越基于深度的基线——在Gibson上100步序列取得88%的0.1m顺序定位成功率，而基线仅68%；在结构不可见场景中则主动弃权。

Conclusion: 几何优先的线框方案能有效替代脆弱的深度网络用于室内定位，在可见墙体几何下表现更优，并能通过弃权机制处理结构盲区场景，为室内定位提供了更鲁棒的替代路径。

Abstract: Floorplans are compact, appearance-invariant maps ideal for indoor localization, yet existing methods rely on depth networks that are brittle in cluttered scenes. We propose GALoc, a geometry-first framework that replaces depth prediction with gravity-aligned wireframes that satisfy verticality and coplanarity by construction. Given monocular RGB, camera intrinsics, relative poses, and IMU orientation, GALoc constructs a linear constraint matrix encoding verticality and coplanarity, and finds the camera gauge minimizing its smallest singular value via global search. The rectified wireframes are projected into bird's-eye-view layouts through a closed-form, FOV-consistent transformation and matched against the floorplan via metric-free SE(2) search. We evaluate end-to-end on Structured3D, with calibrated noise on Gibson, and on real-world author-collected sequences. When sufficient wall geometry is visible, GALoc matches or outperforms depth-based baselines -- achieving 88% sequential localization success at 0.1m over 100-step sequences on Gibson vs the baseline's 68% -- while abstaining in structure-blind scenes.

</details>


### [238] [From Coordinates to Candidate Regions: Temporal Change Localization via Region Selection in Remote Sensing Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08391)
*Juwan Chung,Sungjune Park,Yeongyun Kim,Yong Man Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将自然图像MLLM中的区域选择范式（region selection paradigm）适配到遥感领域，并扩展至时序变化定位任务。通过文本条件区域提议模块生成候选区域，将每个候选编码为携带空间时序视觉特征的特殊token，让LLM通过选择区域token来完成定位，显著优于坐标生成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感多模态大语言模型（RS-MLLMs）虽在场景理解和视觉问答上取得进展，但在定位小且密集的遥感目标或变化区域时面临挑战。现有方法依赖将边界框坐标生成为token序列，这对于遥感中小而密集的目标很脆弱，且多目标定位时错误累积严重。

Method: 提出RS-specific区域选择范式框架：1) 文本条件区域提议模块生成候选区域；2) 每个候选区域编码为携带逐帧视觉特征（融合空间与时间线索）的特殊token；3) LLM通过在响应中选择区域token来实现目标定位；4) 构建涵盖定位、指代表达、视觉grounding和理解任务的多任务训练与评测套件，覆盖单图像与多时序场景。

Result: 实验表明该方法在时序变化定位任务上大幅优于坐标生成基线，同时提升了单图像视觉grounding性能，并保持了有竞争力的理解能力。Oracle分析分别解构了区域提议器和LLM选择器的贡献，提供了该框架独有的诊断性见解。

Conclusion: 区域选择范式是遥感MLLM定位问题的有效替代方案，尤其在时序变化定位和密集小目标场景下优于坐标生成方式，代码已开源。

Abstract: Remote sensing multimodal large language models (RS-MLLMs) have advanced scene understanding and visual question answering over satellite imagery, yet localizing specific objects or changed regions remains challenging. Existing approaches rely on generating bounding box coordinates as token sequences, which is fragile for the small, densely packed objects common in remote sensing and increasingly error-prone when multiple targets must be localized simultaneously. In this work, we present an RS-specific formulation of the region selection paradigm, previously explored in natural-image MLLMs, and extend it to temporal change localization over multi-image sequences. Our framework employs a text-conditioned region proposal module, encodes each candidate as special tokens carrying per-frame visual features enriched with spatial and temporal cues, and lets the LLM localize targets by selecting region tokens in its response. We construct a multi-task training and evaluation suite spanning localization, referring expression, visual grounding, and understanding tasks across single-image and multi-temporal settings. Experiments show that our approach substantially outperforms coordinate-generation baselines on temporal change localization, while improving single-image visual grounding and maintaining competitive understanding performance. Oracle analysis decomposes the contributions of the region proposer and the LLM selector, providing diagnostic insight unique to this framework. Our code will be available at https://github.com/juwan-kr/RS-RegionSelect.

</details>


### [239] [Towards Embodied Air-Ground Cooperative Object Search: Benchmark, Dataset and Agentic Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08402)
*Boao Yu,Zimo Chen,Junreng Rao,Yue Hu,Zhengqiu Zhu,Yong Zhao,Rusheng Ju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AGOS-Bench，这是首个专门评估通用视觉语言模型(VLMs)在城市环境中完成空地目标搜索(AGOS)任务的基准，并配套提供AGOS-Dataset数据集和AGOS-Agent方法。实验表明该方法在九个VLM上大部分提升了成功率并全部减少了决策步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境中的空地目标搜索(AGOS)是一个具有挑战性的具身任务，需要无人机和地面车辆通过多视角视觉参考协同搜索和验证目标车辆。这一问题此前研究不足，缺乏专门的基准来评估VLM在该任务中的表现。

Method: 1) 构建AGOS-Bench基准和AGOS-Dataset数据集(含7.7k个搜索不同类别和属性目标物体的episode，分三个难度级别，由自动化流程构建)；2) 提出AGOS-Agent方法，这是一种无需训练、工具增强的方法，通过"搜索-交接-验证"(search-handoff-verify)协作协议解除VLM处理复杂动态协调的负担，仅要求VLM进行场景理解和决策。

Result: 在九个VLM上的大量实验显示，AGOS-Agent在九个评估骨干中提升了八个的整体成功率，并减少了全部九个的决策步数。在困难子集上，Gemini-3.6-Flash的SR从8.6%提升到55.7%，SPL从7.6%提升到44.0%。

Conclusion: AGOS-Agent通过精心设计的协作协议有效提升了VLM在AGOS任务上的表现，验证了训练免费、工具增强的智能体方法在空地协同搜索中的有效性，为该领域提供了首个标准化基准与可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Air-Ground Object Search (AGOS) in urban environments is a challenging embodied task, which requires an Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) and an Unmanned Ground Vehicle (UGV) to jointly search for and verify a specified target vehicle from multi-view visual references. To study this underexplored problem, we introduce AGOS-Bench, the first dedicated benchmark for evaluating whether general-purpose Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can integrate aerial discoveries and ground-level verification through UAV-UGV cooperation. We further provide AGOS-Dataset as the companion resource of exemplary trajectories constructed by an automatic pipeline. It consists of 7.7k episodes for searching objects of diverse categories and attributes, spanning three difficulty levels. To address the AGOS task, we propose AGOS-Agent, a training-free and tool-augmented approach. The agentic method relieves VLMs from complex and dynamic coordination via a deliberate search-handoff-verify cooperation protocol, only demanding VLMs for scene understanding and decision-making. Extensive experiments on nine VLMs show that AGOS-Agent improves overall success rate for eight of the nine evaluated backbones while reducing decision steps for all nine. On the hard split, the SR and SPL of Gemini-3.6-Flash increase from 8.6% to 55.7% and from 7.6% to 44.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [240] [CAR-MIL: Counterfactual Attention Regularization for Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08419)
*Imane Chraki,Pierre Marza,Stergios Christodoulidis,Maria Vakalopoulou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAR-MIL是一个在标准注意力MIL架构上引入反事实注意力正则化目标的框架，通过训练一个轻量级反事实分支产生与事实注意力分布接近但改变预测的注意力分布，从而提升注意力可靠性并保持分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数MIL方法通过注意力机制聚合实例特征，但注意力权重并不总能真实反映实例重要性，可能聚焦于虚假相关区域，导致证据分配不可靠。

Method: 在标准基于注意力的MIL架构之上，引入一个轻量级反事实注意力分支，训练其产生替代预测但保持与事实注意力分布接近，促使预测变化源于最小化、结构化的注意力再分配。事实与反事实注意力图互补：前者突出支持预测的区域，后者揭示重新加权会挑战预测的区域。

Result: 在带实例级真值的合成MIL基准以及五个数字病理数据集（覆盖四个任务）上评估。CAR-MIL保持有竞争力的分类性能，在更有挑战性的任务上提升最大，同时改善注意力可靠性。

Conclusion: 将反事实可解释性推理整合进注意力学习是有益的，CAR-MIL证明了该方法能提升注意力可靠性且不牺牲分类性能。

Abstract: Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) is widely used for weakly supervised learning, particularly in digital pathology, where fine-grained annotations are costly. Most MIL methods aggregate instance features via attention mechanisms. However, attention weights do not always faithfully reflect instance importance and may focus on spuriously correlated regions. In this work, we propose CAR-MIL, a framework that explicitly guides attention learning through a counterfactual attention regularization objective inspired by counterfactual explanations. Built on a standard attention-based MIL architecture, our approach introduces a lightweight counterfactual attention branch trained to produce an alternative prediction while remaining close to the factual attention distribution. This encourages prediction changes to arise from minimal, structured redistributions of attention, leading to more informative evidence allocation. The resulting factual and counterfactual attention maps capture complementary evidence: the former highlights regions supporting the prediction, while the latter reveals regions whose reweighting would challenge it. We evaluate our method on synthetic MIL benchmarks with instance-level ground truth enabling controlled analysis of attention behavior and on five digital pathology datasets across four tasks. CAR-MIL maintains competitive classification performance, with the largest gains observed on more challenging tasks, while improving attention reliability, demonstrating the benefits of integrating counterfactual explainability reasoning into attention learning. Code is available at: https://github.com/ImaneCR/CAR-MIL/.

</details>


### [241] [AirAnchor: Bridging Local and Global Spatial Information for Zero-Shot Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08442)
*Shanwei Fan,Bin Zhang,Zhiwei Xu,Yingxuan Teng,Siqi Dai,Lin Cheng,Guoliang Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AirAnchor，一种用于航空视觉-语言导航的零样本新范式，通过空间锚点连接局部与全局空间信息，并整合到共享导航框架中，在AerialVLN基准上显著优于现有零样本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有航空视觉-语言导航的零样本方法通常在单一空间尺度上运行，要么仅依赖基于当前观测在线构建的局部表示，要么仅依赖基于历史经验离线构建的全局记忆，缺乏对局部与全局空间信息的综合运用，限制了导航决策的准确性。

Method: AirAnchor包含三个核心组件：(1)查询驱动的空间锚点定位，从视觉观测中识别与决策相关的锚点并组织为局部空间表示；(2)持久目标空间记忆，增量维护目标知识库作为持久全局空间记忆并检索地标相关空间先验；(3)空间感知导航智能体，将局部与全局空间信息显式整合到智能体框架中进行决策。

Result: 在AerialVLN基准上的大量实验表明，AirAnchor大幅优于现有零样本基线方法，验证了该范式的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: AirAnchor通过空间锚点桥接局部和全局空间信息，为航空视觉-语言导航提供了一种更全面的空间感知决策框架，突破了单一空间尺度的局限。

Abstract: Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation requires drones to follow natural-language instructions and navigate through complex urban environments. Accurate navigation relies on both local and global spatial information, which support immediate action grounding and long-horizon path planning, respectively. However, existing zero-shot methods typically operate at a single spatial scale, relying either on local representations constructed online from current observations or on global memories built offline from historical experience. To address this limitation, we propose AirAnchor, a new paradigm that bridges local and global spatial information through spatial anchors and integrates both into a shared navigation framework, enabling comprehensive spatial grounding for decision-making. AirAnchor consists of three core components: (1) Query-Driven Spatial Anchor Grounding, which identifies decision-relevant anchors from visual observations and organizes them into local spatial representations; (2) Persistent Object Spatial Memory, which incrementally maintains an object knowledge base as persistent global spatial memory and retrieves landmark-related spatial priors; and (3) a Spatially-Informed Navigation Agent, which explicitly integrates both local and global spatial information into an agentic framework for decision-making. Extensive experiments on AerialVLN demonstrate that AirAnchor substantially outperforms existing zero-shot baselines, validating the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed paradigm.

</details>


### [242] [GSComplete: Gaussian Splat Completion with 2D Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08449)
*Elias Brugger,Philipp Erler,Stefan Ohrhallinger,Paul Guerrero*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GSComplete是一种基于高斯泼溅的3D物体补全方法，结合Score Distillation Sampling和新的保留损失函数，仅利用2D扩散先验即可完成缺失区域的补全，同时完整保留原有泼溅部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯泼溅虽能提供快速高保真的3D物体表示，但实际输入数据常不完整。现有补全方法要么不保留原始泼溅，要么依赖稀缺的3D训练数据，需要一种既能补全缺失区域又能保持原始数据的方法。

Method: GSComplete将基于Score Distillation Sampling的3D生成与一种新颖的保留损失相结合，该损失鼓励在应可见的位置保留原始泼溅，仅在缺失区域生成新泼溅而不遮挡输入，全程仅依赖2D扩散先验。

Result: 作者提出了一个新的局部高斯泼溅物体数据集用于评估，实验表明GSComplete在输入保留准确性上显著优于现有方法，同时补全结果的合理性相当。

Conclusion: GSComplete证明了无需3D训练数据、仅凭2D扩散先验即可高质量完成高斯泼溅物体补全，同时完美保留原始输入，代码和数据集将在录用后公开。

Abstract: Gaussian splats provide a fast, high-fidelity representation for 3D objects but are often constructed from incomplete input data in practice, leaving missing regions. Existing completion methods either do not preserve the original splats or require scarcely available 3D training data. We propose GSComplete, which combines 3D generation based on Score Distillation Sampling with a novel preservation loss that encourages the original splats to be preserved where they should be visible. This effectively completes the Gaussian splat object using only 2D diffusion priors while fully preserving existing splats and generating new splats only in missing regions, without occluding the input. To evaluate our approach, we introduce a new dataset of partial Gaussian splat objects and show that GSComplete achieves significantly more accurate preservation of the input than existing methods with comparable plausibility of the completed result. Our code and dataset will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [243] [SignRefine: Adapting Foundational Video Models for Sign Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08496)
*Anton Pelykh,Edward Fish,Ozge Mercanoglu Sincan,Richard Bowden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SignRefine，一个基于2D关键点条件生成可理解手语视频的模型，通过局部适配器精准细化手部和面部区域，并引入大规模原生手语视频数据集NVSign以支持训练与更广泛的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型主要基于口语视频训练，生成的手语视频在手部和面部细节上存在伪影，导致手语难以理解。手语视频生成需要精确的手部和面部关节表达，这是现有方法的短板。

Method: SignRefine基于预训练的视频扩散Transformer构建，引入带有空间定位的局部适配器，选择性细化手部和面部区域，引导强基础模型的先验知识朝向精确关节点表达。同时构建NVSign大规模原生手语视频数据集，涵盖多样化的手语者外观、环境和自然对话场景。

Result: 相较最强基线，手部姿态精度指标提升高达30%；在手语用户的视觉质量和可理解性评估中，超过80%的比较中被用户优先选择。

Conclusion: SignRefine证明仅通过2D关键点条件即可生成可理解的手语视频，具备跨外观和视觉条件的泛化能力，为手语视频生成和手语研究提供了新的基准与数据支撑。

Abstract: Sign language video generation demands precise hand and facial articulation, yet modern video diffusion models, trained predominantly on spoken-language video, produce artifacts that render signing unintelligible. We propose SignRefine, a sign language video generation model that produces comprehensible signing from 2D keypoint conditioning alone, generalizing across appearances and visual conditions. Our approach builds on a pretrained video diffusion transformer and introduces local adapters with spatial grounding to selectively refine hand and face regions, steering the strong base model's prior toward accurate articulation. To enable this work and support broader sign language research, we present NVSign, a large-scale dataset of video content natively produced in sign language, offering diverse signer appearances, environments, and natural conversational settings. Trained on this data, our model shows up to 30% improvement in hand pose precision metrics over the strongest baseline and is preferred by sign language users for visual quality and comprehensibility in more than 80% of comparisons.

</details>


### [244] [Temporal State Transport in Video Generation: Diagnosing and Correcting Spectral Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08505)
*Luyao Tang,Bingjun Luo,Dong Yi,Jialin Guo,Haoning Xi,Cheng Chen,Yizhou Yu,Chaoqi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从"时间状态传输"（Temporal State Transport）的视角研究视频生成中的时间一致性。提出一种有符号诊断指标"Spectral Tension"，用于区分两种相反的时间失败模式：碎片化传输与过度混合热点。基于此诊断，提出训练自由的"Spectral Transport Homeostasis"调节器，选择性地纠正病态时间状态，无需微调即可改善时间一致性和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠视频生成不仅需要高质量帧，还需维持连贯的时间叙事，即模型须跨时间传输视觉属性（身份、场景布局、运动、细节）。现有训练自由方法主要加强跨帧注意力或分析局部注意力熵，但无法揭示时间交互是否处于健康的传输状态，缺乏全局视角的诊断工具。

Method: 1) 提出Spectral Tension——一种有符号诊断指标，比较局部注意力扩散度与全局光谱多样性；2) 利用该指标识别两类相反的时间失败：碎片化传输（fragmented transport）和过度混合热点（over-mixing hotspots）；3) 提出Spectral Transport Homeostasis，一种训练自由调节器，通过软校正病态时间状态同时尽量保留平衡状态来改善生成质量。

Result: 在预训练视频生成模型上的实验表明：原始模型通常处于不平衡的时间状态；所提方法能选择性地对最差的时间热点施加更大校正，在不进行微调的情况下提升时间一致性和视觉质量。

Conclusion: 将视频生成时间一致性问题形式化为"时间状态传输"框架，提供了新的诊断视角和训练自由修复方案，无需微调即可显著改善生成质量。

Abstract: Reliable video generation requires more than high-quality frames to form a coherent story: a model must maintain a persistent state, transporting visual attributes such as identity, scene layout, motion, and fine details across time. Existing training-free methods mainly strengthen cross-frame attention or analyze local attention entropy, but these views do not reveal whether temporal interactions stay in a healthy transport regime. In this work, we study video generation through the perspective of Temporal State Transport. We introduce Spectral Tension, a signed diagnostic that compares local attention diffuseness with global spectral diversity, and use it to identify two opposite temporal failures: fragmented transport and over-mixing hotspots. Based on this diagnosis, we propose Spectral Transport Homeostasis, a training-free regulator that softly corrects pathological temporal states while largely preserving balanced ones. Experiments on pretrained video generation models show that the original model often occupies imbalanced temporal regimes, whereas our method selectively applies larger corrections to the worst temporal hotspots and improves temporal consistency and visual quality without finetuning. Code: https://github.com/lytang63/temporal-state-transport

</details>


### [245] [Layer Selection in VLMs for Zero-Shot OOD Detection via Multi-Resolution Entropy Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08524)
*Shyam Nandan Rai,Francesco Di Salvo,Sebastian Doerrich,Christian Ledig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多分辨率熵估计的中间层选择策略，用于医学影像中基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的零样本分布外（OOD）检测。研究发现中间层而非仅最终层能提供互补的OOD信号，且最优层深度因医学影像模态而异。通过聚合多个离散化尺度的直方图统计，解决了单分辨率熵估计对分箱选择敏感的问题（AUROC波动高达19.3%），在MIDOG和OASIS两个医学OOD基准上稳定超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的零样本OOD检测方法几乎完全依赖最终层嵌入，隐含假设最深层的表示普遍最优。然而在医学影像中，这一假设并不成立——中间层能提供互补的OOD信号，且最优表示深度取决于具体影像模态。此外，先前工作通过熵最小化选择层组合时，单分辨率熵估计对分箱选择高度敏感，性能波动可达19.3% AUROC，亟需更稳定的层选择方法。

Method: 提出一种多分辨率熵估计策略：在多个离散化尺度上聚合直方图统计信息，以鲁棒且稳定地选择VLM的中间层组合用于OOD检测。该方法利用跨模态相似度作为非参数置信信号，并通过多分辨率直方图熵估计来克服单分辨率估计对分箱参数的敏感性，从而实现稳定的零样本OOD检测。

Result: 在MIDOG和OASIS两个覆盖不同成像模态、多种偏移类型和不同VLM骨干网络的医学OOD基准上，所提方法在性能上一致优于现有最先进方法，同时保持轻量级和稳定性。

Conclusion: 中间层表示对医学影像OOD检测具有重要价值，多分辨率熵估计为中间层选择提供了稳定且轻量的解决方案，为医学AI系统的安全部署提供了更可靠的零样本OOD检测手段。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is crucial for safe deployment of medical AI systems, where domain shifts arise across institutions, acquisition protocols, and patient populations. VLMs enable zero-shot OOD detection by embedding images into a language-aligned latent space, where cross-modal similarity serves as a non-parametric confidence signal for identifying in-distribution samples. Yet existing methods rely almost exclusively on final-layer embeddings, implicitly assuming that the deepest representations are universally optimal. We first show that this assumption does not hold in medical imaging: intermediate layers provide complementary OOD signals, and the optimal representational depth depends on the respective image modality. While prior work selects layer combinations via entropy minimization of normalized histograms, we demonstrate that single-resolution entropy estimation is highly sensitive to binning choices, leading to performance variations of up to 19.3% AUROC. To address this instability, we propose a multi-resolution entropy estimation strategy that aggregates histogram statistics across multiple discretization scales, enabling robust and stable intermediate-layer selection. Across two medical OOD benchmarks, namely MIDOG and OASIS, covering distinct imaging modalities, diverse shift types, and different VLM backbones, our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, offering a lightweight and stable solution for zero-shot OOD detection.

</details>


### [246] [STSG-VQA: Evidence-Grounded Temporal Question Answering from Surgical Spatio-Temporal Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08543)
*Jing Li,Duygu Sarikaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多层级结构化时间监督方法，通过增强框架级手术场景图为包含对象连续性、事件级交互连续性和流程级连通性的时空场景图（STSG），并基于此构建STSG-VQA基准数据集（含18,458个问答对，覆盖7个时间类别），用于提升手术视觉语言模型的时间推理能力。实验表明，基于STSG监督微调Qwen3-VL-4B和Hulu-Med-4B，相比零样本基线分别提升24.39和19.56个百分点，相比静态场景图监督分别提升16.50和14.25个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视觉语言模型（VLM）的时间推理能力有限，因为现有监督主要基于帧层面。虽然帧级场景图（SG）能提供手术环境的结构化表示，但无法显式建模手术工作流的动态演变过程。

Method: 提出多层级结构化时间监督方法，将帧级手术场景图增强为时空场景图（STSG），具体包括三个层面：1) 对象级连续性；2) 事件级交互连续性；3) 流程级连通性。随后在STSG上执行时间查询以生成有证据支撑的问答对，形成STSG-VQA基准，每个问题都关联到时间区间和STSG证据，支持可追溯验证。

Result: 基准包含18,458个问答对，覆盖7个时间类别。微调Qwen3-VL-4B和Hulu-Med-4B后，问题级微准确率较零样本基线分别提升24.39和19.56个百分点，较静态场景图监督分别提升16.50和14.25个百分点，且所有时间类别均有增益。

Conclusion: STSG衍生监督能帮助手术VLM对时间导向的交互进行推理，而非局限于孤立帧。该工作将代码和数据集在论文被接收后公开发布。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in surgical vision-language models (VLMs), temporal reasoning remains limited because existing supervision is largely frame-centric. Frame-level scene graphs (SGs) have proven effective in providing structured representations of surgical environments but do not explicitly model the dynamics of surgical workflows. To explicitly model how surgical states evolve across time, we introduce a multi-level structured temporal supervision methodology that augments frame-level surgical SGs with object-level continuity, event-level interaction continuity, and procedure-level connectivity. We then execute temporal queries over the resulting spatio-temporal scene graphs (STSGs) to generate evidence-grounded question-answer pairs, which together form the STSG-VQA benchmark. Each question is linked to the temporal interval and STSG evidence used to derive its reference answer, enabling traceable verification. The benchmark contains 18,458 question-answer pairs across seven temporal categories. Fine-tuning Qwen3-VL-4B and Hulu-Med-4B with STSG-derived supervision improves question-level micro accuracy by 24.39 and 19.56 percentage points over their zero-shot baselines and by 16.50 and 14.25 points over static scene-graph supervision, respectively. These gains span all temporal categories, indicating that STSG-derived supervision helps surgical VLMs reason over temporally grounded interactions rather than isolated frames. The code and dataset will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [247] [Effects of model architecture and learning strategies on deep learning-based recognition of activated sludge microscopic images and comparison with quantitative image analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08570)
*Suguru Hakoshima,Tomohiro Tobino,Fumiyuki Nakajima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了深度学习在活性污泥显微图像分类中的性能，比较了Transformer与CNN架构、不同预训练策略及下采样方案，并首次将深度学习与定量图像分析（QIA）进行定量对比，结果显示Transformer架构和替代预训练方法更有效，保持视野比保持分辨率的下采样策略更优，且深度学习在准确率上全面超越QIA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往活性污泥显微图像分析研究多依赖CNN和有监督ImageNet预训练，鲜有探索Transformer或自监督基础模型；且图像下采样对性能的影响未被系统研究；同时尚无研究定量比较深度学习与传统的定量图像分析（QIA）方法。

Method: 制备三种活性污泥样本，对其显微图像进行分类，通过改变模型架构（CNN vs Transformer）和预训练策略（ImageNet vs 替代方法）评估分类准确率；系统研究不同下采样方案（保持视野 vs 保持分辨率）的影响；并将深度学习结果与QIA进行定量对比。

Result: Transformer架构和替代预训练方法在分类准确率上表现更优；图像过小会降低准确率，但增大到一定程度后性能不再提升；保持视野比保持分辨率是更有效的下采样策略；深度学习在准确率上优于QIA。

Conclusion: 深度学习架构选择和下采样策略对活性污泥显微图像分类性能有显著影响，深度学习相比传统QIA方法更具优势，为该领域后续研究提供了架构选择与预处理策略的指导。

Abstract: Microscopic image analysis has long been recognized as a promising approach for monitoring activated sludge. In recent years, deep learning-based image analysis has been increasingly adopted in this field because of its high performance. However, previous studies on microscopic image analysis of activated sludge have rarely explored transformer-based models or self-supervised foundation models and have instead relied on CNNs and supervised ImageNet pretraining. In addition, previous studies often downsampled image sizes, but the effects of downsampling have not been sufficiently investigated, and the relationship between downsampling strategies and image analysis performance remains unclear. Furthermore, no study has quantitatively compared deep learning performance with quantitative image analysis (QIA), which was widely used before the emergence of deep learning. In this study, to examine how model architecture and learning strategies affect performance in microscopic image analysis of activated sludge and to quantitatively determine whether deep learning outperforms QIA, we prepared three types of activated sludge samples, classified their microscopic images, and evaluated classification accuracy. Our results showed that transformer-based architectures and alternative pretraining methods were effective in terms of classification accuracy. Our downsampling analysis showed that using overly small images reduced accuracy, but increasing image size beyond a certain point did not improve it further. In addition, the analysis indicated that, to achieve high classification accuracy, maintaining the field of view was a more effective downsampling strategy than maintaining resolution. Finally, our comparison between deep learning and QIA showed that deep learning outperformed QIA in terms of accuracy.

</details>


### [248] [GOLF: Global Observation with Local Focus for Calibration-Aware Stereo Interaction Field Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08607)
*Minqiang Zou,Riqiang Jin,Zhi Lv,Dong Luo,Lianghai Tian,Zhenyu Zhao,Qi Xu,Tong Wu,Mochen Yu,Yao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GOLF是SHOW3D交互场估计挑战赛的第一名方案，利用同步的自我中心立体视图，从21个手关节预测指向操作对象最近点的3D向量。该方法结合稠密全局上下文、局部采样手部/物体证据和公共坐标系中的Plücker射线几何，并采用带LoRA和可训练LayerNorm的DINOv3 ViT-H+/16主干进行联合解码。主模型在隐藏测试集上取得了27.61的官方分数和27.96 mm的平均ADE，通过等权集成进一步提升了性能（27.47分，27.82 mm ADE），最终夺得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手-物交互场估计对于理解人与物体的交互至关重要，但该任务面临从立体视图准确预测手关节到物体的3D距离向量的挑战，需要同时处理全局语义背景、局部手物证据以及跨视图几何一致性。

Method: 提出的GOLF方法包括三个关键组件：(1) 利用DINOv3 ViT-H+/16作为骨干网络，结合LoRA和可训练LayerNorm参数进行适应；(2) 融合稠密全局上下文与局部采样得到的手部和物体证据；(3) 通过公共坐标系中的Plücker射线几何来统一多视图信息，并对两个交互场进行联合解码。

Result: 主模型在隐藏测试集上获得27.61的官方分数和27.96 mm的平均ADE；与直接微调的互补变体进行等权集成后，官方分数提升至27.47，平均ADE降至27.82 mm，夺得了挑战赛第一名。

Conclusion: GOLF验证了结合全局上下文、局部证据与几何约束的方法在手-物交互场估计中的有效性，并表明通过集成不同训练策略的互补模型可以进一步取得性能提升，最终在该挑战中取得领先地位。

Abstract: We present GOLF, the first-place solution to the SHOW3D Interaction Field Estimation Challenge at HANDS@ECCV 2026. Given synchronized egocentric stereo views, the task is to predict a 3D vector from each of 21 hand joints to the closest point on the manipulated object. GOLF combines dense global context, locally sampled hand/object evidence, and common-frame Plücker-ray geometry. We adapt DINOv3 ViT-H+/16 with LoRA and trainable LayerNorm parameters, then jointly decode both interaction fields. Our primary model achieves an official score of 27.61 and a mean ADE of 27.96 mm on the hidden test set. An equal-weight ensemble with a complementary directly fine-tuned variant improves these results to an official score of 27.47 and a mean ADE of 27.82 mm, securing first place.

</details>


### [249] [SynthRCT: Scalable Conditional Deformation Synthesis for Synthetic Repeat CT Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08627)
*Tomas Guija-Valiente,Blanca Rodriguez-Gonzalez,Norberto Malpica*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SynthRCT，一种基于条件变分自编码器的可扩展条件生成框架，用于合成3D解剖变形。该方法学习潜在变形空间，将采样的潜在编码解码为局部平稳速度场，并组装成完整的全卷变换，以支持大视野CT数据的内存可扩展生成。在呼吸4DCT数据上验证，能够实现患者特定的合理解剖变换采样，超越预定义的鲁棒性场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子治疗中计划通常在单一计划CT上优化，因此需要在解剖变化下进行鲁棒性评估。然而现有场景通常依赖简化的扰动，难以捕捉复杂的、患者特定的变异性，亟需更真实的解剖变形合成方法。

Method: 基于条件变分自编码器(CVAE)的生成框架SynthRCT：学习潜在变形空间，将采样的潜在编码解码为以输入解剖为条件的局部平稳速度场(LSVF)，再将局部场组装为完整的全卷变换，实现大视野CT数据的内存可扩展生成。

Result: 在包含每位受试者多个呼吸相位解剖的呼吸4DCT数据上验证了该方法，表明SynthRCT能够实现患者特定的合理解剖变换采样，超越预定义的鲁棒性场景。

Conclusion: SynthRCT为质子治疗鲁棒性评估提供了可扩展且患者特定的解剖变形合成方案，补充了现有基于简化扰动的评估方法的不足。

Abstract: In proton therapy, plans are typically optimized on a single planning CT, making robustness evaluation essential under anatomical changes. However, current scenarios often rely on simplified perturbations that poorly capture complex, patient-specific variability. We propose SynthRCT, a scalable conditional generative framework for 3D anatomical deformation synthesis. Based on a conditional variational autoencoder, SynthRCT learns a latent deformation space and decodes sampled latent codes into local stationary velocity fields conditioned on an input anatomy. Local fields are assembled into coherent full-volume transformations, enabling memory-scalable generation for large field-of-view CT data. We validate the approach on respiratory 4DCT data with multiple breathing-phase anatomies per subject. SynthRCT enables patient-specific sampling of plausible anatomical transformations beyond predefined robustness scenarios. Code available at: https://github.com/TomasGuija/SynthRCT.

</details>


### [250] [From Where to How: Continuous 4D Interaction Forecasting from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08636)
*Qiaohui Chu,Haoyu Zhang,Meng Liu,Haoxiang Shi,Dongmei Jiang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Coherent4D大规模自我中心4D交互预测数据集（约23.3万样本，覆盖三个领域），并构建HIGFlow框架，采用级联"where-to-how"过程：先预测连续3D交互位置，再用预测位置序列引导确定性运动锚点结合残差流匹配生成多样且结构一致的全身体姿预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以将语义理解转化为精确的连续3D定位，难以在姿态预测中平衡动作多样性与结构一致性；更根本的是，交互位置与人体运动常被分开建模，导致两者间连续的几何与时间对应关系未被充分捕获。

Method: 1) 构建Coherent4D数据集，将未来3D交互位置序列与对应全身姿态配对，在共享坐标系中对齐时间。2) 提出HIGFlow框架，采用级联的where-to-how过程：先通过语义基础与短期视觉动态预测连续交互位置，再利用位置序列条件化确定性运动锚点和残差流匹配，实现多样且结构一致的全身体姿预测。

Result: 在三个领域的广泛实验中，HIGFlow在位置预测与姿态预测上均较代表性基线取得一致提升，消融实验验证了各提出组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过将"在哪里交互"与"如何运动"统一为级联建模框架并配套大规模连续空间数据集，能够更充分地捕获交互位置与人体运动之间的几何和时间对应关系，为辅助机器人与人机交互提供重要能力。

Abstract: Egocentric 4D interaction forecasting aims to anticipate both where future interactions will occur in 3D and how the human body will move to realize them, providing an important capability for assistive robotics and human-computer interaction. Existing methods struggle to translate semantic understanding into precise continuous 3D localization and to balance motion diversity with structural consistency in pose forecasting. More fundamentally, these tasks are often modeled separately, leaving the continuous geometric and temporal correspondence between interaction locations and body motion insufficiently captured. To address these challenges, we introduce Coherent4D, a large-scale egocentric dataset for continuous 4D interaction forecasting, comprising approximately 233K samples across three domains. Each sample pairs a sequence of future 3D interaction locations with corresponding full-body poses, aligned in time and expressed in a shared coordinate system. We also provide evaluation metrics in continuous space. Building on this formulation, we propose HIGFlow, a Hand Interaction Guided Residual Flow framework that models forecasting as a cascaded where-to-how process. HIGFlow first forecasts continuous future interaction locations by combining semantic grounding with short-horizon visual dynamics, and then uses the predicted location sequence to condition a deterministic motion anchor and residual Flow Matching for diverse yet structurally consistent full-body motion forecasting. Extensive experiments across all three domains demonstrate consistent improvements over representative baselines on both location and pose forecasting, while ablations validate the contributions of the proposed components. The project page is available at https://corrineqiu.github.io/from-where-to-how/.

</details>


### [251] [Charts Are Beyond Pixels: Probing for Layer-Wise Chart Understanding and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08657)
*Xiaochuan Zhong,Yifan Hou,Chenxi Pang,Shaobo Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LayerWiseBench基准，从图层层面评估多模态大模型对图表的理解与编辑能力，涵盖图层归因、图层绑定与可见性排序三个核心概念，并通过实验发现模型在前景/背景重叠关系的处理上存在系统性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表基准主要评估最终输出的正确性或保真度，未能直接评估模型在图层层面（layer-level）对图表结构化元素的理解与操作能力，而图表的组成元素具有独特的功能角色、语义对应关系和可见性关系，这种结构性视角需要专门的评估方法。

Method: 从可执行的图表程序生成图表，为每个渲染图表配配对空间对齐的逐层RGBA资产及构建派生的标签（功能角色、语义绑定、可见性关系），由此推出受控的理解问题、编辑目标、参考图像和评估区域。构建14种图表范式下的2800个源图表，衍生出7,329个图层级理解问题和53,791个指令引导编辑变体。

Result: 评估的VLM中，Qwen3.5-27B在QA宏平均值上最高，图层归因准确率93.04%，图层绑定97.46%，但可见性排序仅61.46%。四个图像编辑器的整体mIoU范围为1.49%至4.93%，其中可见性约束编辑的mIoU最低（0.37%-2.00%）。

Conclusion: 重叠组件之间的前后关系（front-to-back relations）在理解与编辑任务中都是持续存在的挑战，这促使模型需要对组件身份和可见性关系进行更显式的建模。

Abstract: Charts are structured visual compositions whose elements have distinct functional roles, semantic correspondences, and visibility relations. This structural view motivates evaluating whether models can understand and manipulate charts at the layer level. Existing chart benchmarks, however, primarily assess the correctness or fidelity of final outputs and do not directly evaluate these layer-wise behaviors. We present LayerWiseBench, a benchmark organized around three core concepts, layer attribution, layer binding, and visibility ordering, that structure its chart-understanding and chart-editing evaluations. Generated from executable chart programs, LayerWiseBench pairs each rendered chart with spatially aligned per-layer RGBA assets and construction-derived labels for functional roles, semantic bindings, and visibility relations. From this layer-wise representation, we derive controlled understanding questions, editing targets, reference images, and evaluation regions. It contains 2,800 source charts across 14 chart paradigms, from which we derive 7,329 layer-wise understanding questions and 53,791 instruction-guided editing variants. Among the evaluated VLMs, Qwen3.5-27B, which achieves the highest QA macro-average, obtains 93.04% accuracy on layer attribution and 97.46% on layer binding, but only 61.46% on visibility ordering. Across the four evaluated image editors, overall mIoU ranges from 1.49% to 4.93%, and visibility-constrained edits have the lowest mIoU for every editor, ranging from 0.37% to 2.00%. Taken together, these results identify tasks involving front-to-back relations between overlapping components as a recurring challenge across understanding and editing, motivating more explicit modeling of component identity and visibility relations.

</details>


### [252] [TriCCOT: Tri-part Convolutional Conformal Transformer for Onboard Space Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08659)
*Adrien Dorise,Marjorie Bellizzi,Julia Cohen,Stéphane May*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriCCOT是一种三部分架构，专为星载（onboard）目标检测设计，兼顾鲁棒性与FPGA可部署性。它结合了卷积区域提议网络、一致预测（conformal prediction）阶段和Aper-GATES（硬件友好的注意力分类器），在DIOR和VDVRaw数据集上展现出竞争力的检测性能和更强的抗模糊/抗噪鲁棒性，并成功部署于Xilinx Versal VCK190 FPGA上，无需修改底层DPU架构即可实现统一的CNN-Transformer推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星载目标检测面临两难：卷积检测器硬件高效但难以从原始含噪数据中提取鲁棒特征；Transformer模型全局推理能力强但因其二次注意力复杂度和不兼容操作难以部署于FPGA。作者希望构建一种既能从原始噪声图像中提取强特征，又能在CNN导向的FPGA加速器上高效部署的目标检测架构。

Method: TriCCOT采用三部分架构：(1)卷积区域提议网络用于生成候选边界框；(2)一致预测阶段对边界框进行扩展，提供无分布假设的概率覆盖率保证；(3)Aper-GATES分类器，通过卷积投影、全局通道统计和硬件友好的门控操作重新定义自注意力，避免标准Transformer中与CNN加速器不兼容的操作。

Result: 在DIOR和VDVRaw数据集上，TriCCOT相对于FPGA兼容架构展现了有竞争力的检测性能，并对空间模糊和信号相关噪声具有更好的鲁棒性。此外，该方法成功完整部署于Xilinx Versal VCK190 FPGA，无需修改底层DPU架构，实现了星载嵌入式应用中的统一CNN-Transformer推理。

Conclusion: TriCCOT证明了可以在不牺牲FPGA可部署性的前提下，通过硬件友好的注意力机制设计获得Transformer级特征表达能力的可能性。一致预测的引入为星载检测提供了可验证的不确定性量化，整体架构为资源受限的星载环境提供了实用的CNN-Transformer统一推理方案。

Abstract: Onboard object detection in Earth observation is constrained by limited computational resources and the absence of fully corrected imagery. While convolutional detectors are hardware-efficient, they often struggle to extract robust representations from raw and noisy data. Conversely, transformer-based models provide stronger global reasoning capabilities but remain difficult to deploy on FPGA accelerators due to quadratic attention complexity and non-compatible operations.
  We introduce TriCCOT, a tri-part architecture for robust and deployable onboard object detection. TriCCOT combines a convolutional region proposal network, a conformal prediction stage, and Aper-GATES, our hardware-friendly attention-based classifier. The region proposal network generates candidate bounding boxes, which are subsequently enlarged via conformal prediction, providing a distribution-free probabilistic coverage guarantee. The resulting crops are processed by Aper-GATES, which reformulates self-attention through convolutional projections, global channel statistics, and hardware-friendly gating operations, avoiding standard transformer operations that are poorly suited to CNN-oriented accelerators.
  Experiments on the DIOR and VDVRaw datasets demonstrate competitive detection performance and improved robustness to spatial blur and signal-dependent noise when compared to FPGA-compatible architectures. Finally, we report full deployment on a Xilinx Versal VCK190 FPGA without modifying the underlying DPU architecture, enabling unified CNN-Transformer inference for spaceborne embedded applications.

</details>


### [253] [CoordFormer: Give Me Any Coordinates and I Will Give You Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08660)
*Iacopo Curti,Pierluigi Zama Ramirez,Alioscia Petrelli,Luigi Di Stefano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoordFormer是一种基于坐标的语义分割架构，通过Coordinate Decoder和Localized Cross-Attention机制，在超高清图像上实现任意空间位置的标签预测，兼顾丰富的语义上下文与像素级精度，同时支持边界聚焦策略降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高清图像上的语义分割面临两大挑战：计算成本高昂以及难以捕捉细粒度细节。现有方法难以同时兼顾高分辨率输入的效率与精度。

Method: 提出CoordFormer架构，包含：(1) Coordinate Decoder，结合坐标嵌入与高分辨率局部patch特征；(2) Localized Cross-Attention机制，用于交互全局token与局部特征；(3) 使用ViT基础编码器处理降采样图像提取全局语义上下文；(4) 支持语义边缘聚焦策略，将计算集中在边界区域以降低延迟。

Result: CoordFormer在MaSS13K上达到最先进性能，在DIS5K和KPIs上超越同参数量级及更高参数量的现有方法，证明了其在高质量超高清语义分割中的有效性。

Conclusion: CoordFormer通过坐标解码器与局部交叉注意力机制的设计，成功在超高清语义分割中实现了计算效率与细粒度精度之间的良好平衡，为高分辨率图像分割提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation on very-high-resolution images remains challenging due to the high computational cost and the difficulty of capturing fine-grained details. We propose CoordFormer, a novel coordinate-based architecture for semantic segmentation that predicts labels at arbitrary spatial locations through a Coordinate Decoder equipped with a Localized Cross-Attention mechanism. The decoder combines coordinate embeddings with high-resolution local patch features and interacts with global tokens extracted from a downsampled image processed by a ViT foundation encoder, enabling rich semantic context while preserving pixel-level precision. This design enables flexible inference at arbitrary resolutions while keeping memory low on very-high-resolution inputs, and supports an efficient semantic-edge-focused strategy that concentrates computation along boundaries, maintaining fine-grained accuracy while reducing latency and computational cost. CoordFormer achieves state-of-the-art performance on MaSS13K and outperforms comparably sized and higher-parameter methods on DIS5K and KPIs, demonstrating its effectiveness for high-quality, very-high-resolution semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [254] [CausalChapter: Improving Long-Video Chaptering with Interventional Dependency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08686)
*Xinran Duan,Guozhang Li,Yaoyao Zhong,Mei Wang,Lizhi Wang,Hua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalChapter框架，通过轻量级遮蔽和移除干预来估计预测级影响，解决长视频章节划分中"段-标题"范式的两个挑战：边界误差传播和跨章节上下文碎片化。包含Local Dependency Shift模块（边界定位）和Cross-Segment Support Selection模块（章节描述生成），在基准测试上提升了边界定位、章节描述质量和跨章节连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长格式教学视频需要自动章节划分以支持浏览、导航和知识获取。虽然长上下文语言模型可以处理文本化的视频输入，但它们在内容密集的讲座视频上成本高昂且脆弱（长转录、平滑主题转换、详细章节输出）。段-标题范式虽能降低成本，却引入了边界误差传播和碎片化跨章节上下文两个新问题。

Method: 提出CausalChapter干预启发式框架：(1) Local Dependency Shift模块通过检测相邻时间窗口间预测依赖性的下降来定位边界；(2) Cross-Segment Support Selection模块根据历史上下文对当前预测的支持程度进行重排序，用于生成章节描述。整体通过轻量级遮蔽和移除干预估计预测级因果关系影响。

Result: 在长视频章节划分基准测试上的实验表明，CausalChapter显著提升了边界定位精度、章节描述质量以及跨章节连贯性。

Conclusion: CausalChapter通过引入因果干预思想，有效解决了段-标题范式中边界误差传播和跨章节上下文碎片化问题，为长视频自动章节化提供了一种可扩展且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-form instructional videos require automatic chaptering to support browsing, navigation, and knowledge access. Recent long-context language models can perform chaptering from textualized video inputs, but they remain costly and brittle for content-dense lecture videos with long transcripts, smooth topic transitions, and detailed chapter outputs. A scalable segment-then-caption paradigm reduces this cost, but introduces two new challenges: boundary error propagation and fragmented cross-chapter context. We propose \textbf{CausalChapter}, an intervention-inspired framework for long-video chaptering that estimates prediction-level influence through lightweight masking and removal interventions. For boundary localization, our Local Dependency Shift module detects drops in predictive dependency between adjacent temporal windows; for chapter description generation, our Cross-Segment Support Selection module reranks historical contexts according to their support for the current prediction. Experiments on long-video chaptering benchmarks show that CausalChapter improves boundary localization, chapter description quality, and cross-chapter coherence.

</details>


### [255] [Enhancing Table Structure Recognition via Bounding Box Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08705)
*Lei Hu,Shuangping Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BGTR框架，先预测单元格边界框，再用边界框信息引导HTML序列生成，以提升表格结构识别（TSR）在复杂场景下的准确率。针对自然场景表格数据不足的问题，采用渐进式训练方法并引入合成数据集SNSTab，在五个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到序列的TSR方法在预测HTML序列时忽视了显式利用边界框信息，导致复杂场景下出现错误预测。

Method: 提出BGTR（Bounding Box-Guided Table Recognizer）框架：首先预测单元格边界框，再利用该信息引导HTML序列生成；采用渐进式训练方法应对自然场景表格数据量不足的问题，并构建合成自然场景表格数据集SNSTab。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上取得了最先进的（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: 将边界框信息显式引入HTML序列预测流程能有效提升TSR在复杂场景下的准确性，合成数据与渐进式训练是缓解自然场景数据稀缺的有效策略。

Abstract: Table Structure Recognition (TSR) aims to extract the bounding boxes of cells and table structure (e.g., HTML) from table images. Although current approaches have made significant progress, the latest image-to-sequence methods overlook the explicit utilization of the bounding box information when predicting HTML sequences, leading to error predictions in complex scenes. In this paper, we introduce a novel framework BGTR (Bounding Box-Guided Table Recognizer). To more effectively utilize bounding box information, we first predict the bounding boxes of cells and then use this information to guide the generation of HTML sequences. While utilizing bounding box information can enhance the accuracy of HTML sequences, for natural scene tables, the data volume is too small to allow for sufficient training of bbox-guided HTML generation. In response, we adopt a progressive training method for natural scene tables and introduce SNSTab, a synthetically generated natural scene table dataset. Our experiments on five benchmark datasets demonstrate SOTA performance.

</details>


### [256] [CVT-GS: Learning to Simplify 3D Gaussian Splatting with Centroidal Voronoi Tessellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08730)
*Bingxian Li,Yilong Li,Jingliang Peng,Peng-Shuai Wang,Fei Zhu,Guozheng Li,Chi Harold Liu,Guoping Wang,Bo Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CVT-GS，一种无需优化的后处理3DGS简化框架，通过几何感知的Centroidal Voronoi Tessellation构建空间单元，并用轻量级神经单元合并器将每个单元中的多个高斯原语合并为单个高代表性高斯原语，实现大规模压缩且保持渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS渲染高质量场景依赖大量高斯原语，造成巨大的存储和计算开销。现有简化技术多为侵入式，需要训练时剪枝、架构修改或昂贵的逐场景微调，难以直接应用于现成的预训练模型。

Method: 1) 利用几何感知的CVT在高斯中心上构建空间连贯单元；2) 设计轻量级神经单元合并器，在可微渲染监督下为每个单元预测单个高代表性高斯原语的几何和外观属性；3) 将简化建模为渲染感知的多对一合并过程，而非简单剪枝，输出与现有渲染器兼容的标准3DGS场景。

Result: 在多种数据集上验证了方法的优越性。当高斯点数减少100倍时，运行速度比最先进方法快12倍，同时PSNR提升了1.3 dB。

Conclusion: CVT-GS通过优化自由的后处理压缩框架，成功解决了现有3DGS简化方法的侵入性问题，实现了大幅压缩的同时保持视觉保真度，并对现成预训练模型具有良好兼容性。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a powerful representation for real-time novel view synthesis, rendering high-fidelity scenes often relies on a massive number of Gaussian primitives, incurring substantial storage and computational overhead. Existing simplification techniques are largely intrusive, requiring training-time pruning, architectural modifications, or computationally expensive per-scene fine-tuning. These drawbacks limit their deployment on off-the-shelf pretrained models. In this paper, we propose CVT-GS, a novel optimization-free post-hoc simplification framework that directly compresses trained 3DGS scenes without sacrificing visual fidelity. Our approach first constructs spatially coherent cells over Gaussian centers via a geometry-aware Centroidal Voronoi Tessellation (CVT). Subsequently, a lightweight neural cell merger predicts the geometry and appearance of a single, highly representative Gaussian primitive for each cell under differentiable rendering supervision. By formulating simplification as a rendering-aware many-to-one merging process rather than naive primitive pruning, CVT-GS outputs a standard 3DGS scene that is seamlessly compatible with existing renderers. Experiments on various datasets demonstrate the superiority of our method. Notably, when achieving a 100-fold reduction in Gaussian points, our method operates 12 times faster than state-of-the-art methods while improving the PSNR by 1.3 dB.

</details>


### [257] [Kairos: A Dataset for Fine-Grained Video-Language Modeling over Space, Time, and Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08755)
*Ruibo Ming,Lei Sun,Deheng Zhang,He Zhang,Jialu Li,Jian Wang,Zhendong Li,Mengshun Hu,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Kairos是一个用于视频语言建模的长视频数据集，包含10分钟至半小时的长视频，具备细粒度的时间对齐标注，涵盖持续动作、实体外观与属性、交互及上下文线索沿时间线的演化，支持细粒度评估、长程建模与推理、指令数据构建、表征学习和视频生成等多种任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频数据集大多依赖粗粒度或稀疏对齐的监督，压缩了时间变化，限制了模型学习连续视觉动态的可复用表征的能力，无法满足长时间跨度的视频语言建模需求。

Method: 构建Kairos数据集，包含长时间（10分钟至半小时）视频，并以细粒度时间对齐方式标注持续动作、实体出现与属性、交互以及演化的上下文线索。

Result: Kairos作为通用基础数据集，支持细粒度评估、长距离建模与推理、指令数据构建、表征学习以及视频生成等多项下游任务。

Conclusion: Kairos提供了一个通用基础，用于时间维度上的视觉经验建模，弥合了现有数据集在时间解析度上的不足。

Abstract: Many emerging video language modeling tasks require systems to move beyond clip-level abstraction and model visual content as it unfolds over extended time horizons. However, most existing video datasets rely on coarse or sparsely aligned supervision, which compresses temporal variation and limits the ability of models to learn reusable representations of continuous visual dynamics. We introduce Kairos, a video dataset for video-language modeling with time-resolved annotations. Kairos consists of long-duration videos, ranging from ten minutes to half an hour, annotated with fine-grained temporal alignment. The annotations capture ongoing actions, entity appearances and attributes, interactions, and evolving contextual cues along the video timeline. This time-resolved structure supports fine-grained evaluation, long-range modeling and reasoning, instruction data construction, representation learning, and video generation. Kairos provides a general-purpose foundation for modeling visual experiences over time.

</details>


### [258] [Compensating for Scarce Historical Images in Cross-Domain Cultural Heritage Retrieval Using Synthetic Aging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08766)
*Marcin Iwanowski,Adam Mazgaj,Ferdynand Gorski,Sabina Szymoniak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨在文化遗产双向量级检索中，用合成老化当代图像替代或补充稀缺的真实历史图像训练数据的可行性。结果显示完全替代会降低性能（R@1从86.56%降至81.27%），但在真实历史图像稀缺时（25%-75%覆盖率），合成补充可带来2-3.69个百分点的提升，表明真实与合成数据具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产中同一物理对象的当代与历史视觉记录往往稀缺且难以关联，主要因为视角、采集条件、颜色复制、分辨率及退化程度差异大，且真实历史图像数量有限。作者希望通过合成老化方法生成当代图像的"旧域"版本，以补充或替代稀缺的训练数据，提升跨域实例级检索性能。

Method: 采用退化导向变换生成合成老域图像；使用EfficientNetV2-M模型，在身份不重叠的训练/验证/测试集上，跨三个数据集分区与三个训练种子进行评估；对比混合真实-合成训练与纯真实基线，采用等比缩放和固定300批/epoch两种训练调度；考察不同真实历史覆盖率（25%/50%/75%）下合成补充的效果，并测试不同数量合成变体是否带来增益。

Result: 完全替代真实历史图像时，双向均值R@1从86.56%降至81.27%；增加合成变体数量无一致改善；在受控稀缺条件下，合成补充在25%覆盖率时提升3.69个百分点，50%时提升2.92，75%时降至2.00，接近完整数据参考水平；固定调度纯真实对照组无法复现该提升。

Conclusion: 真实与合成观测具有互补性而非替代关系。合成补充主要通过扩展跨域身份覆盖来提高检索性能，而非增加训练暴露量；其贡献随真实历史覆盖率增加而逐渐减弱。合成老化无法完全还原真实旧域的完整变异性。

Abstract: Cultural heritage collections often contain contemporary and historical visual records of the same physical object. Linking these records is difficult because corresponding images may differ in viewpoint, acquisition conditions, color reproduction, framing, resolution, and degradation, while genuine historical images are frequently scarce. This study investigates whether synthetically aged contemporary images can replace or complement missing historical training data in bidirectional instance-level retrieval. Synthetic old-domain images are generated using degradation-oriented transformations. An EfficientNetV2-M model is evaluated on identity-disjoint training, validation, and test sets across three dataset partitions and three training seeds. Mixed real-synthetic training is compared with real-only baselines using proportionally scaled and fixed 300-batch-per-epoch schedules. Complete replacement of genuine historical images reduced bidirectional mean R@1 from 86.56% to 81.27%, showing that synthetic aging does not reproduce the full genuine old-domain variability. Increasing the number of independently generated synthetic variants provided no consistent improvement. Under controlled scarcity, however, synthetic completion improved mean R@1 by 3.69 percentage points at 25% genuine historical coverage and by 2.92 points at 50%, relative to the proportionally scaled real-only baselines. At 75%, the gain decreased to 2.00 points, while performance remained comparable to the complete-real-data reference. Fixed-schedule real-only controls did not reproduce these improvements. The results indicate that genuine and synthetic observations are complementary. Synthetic completion primarily benefits retrieval by extending cross-domain identity coverage rather than by increasing training exposure, with its contribution gradually decreasing as genuine historical coverage increases.

</details>


### [259] [AXS-Net: Interpretable Deep Unfolding for Hyperspectral Image Denoising via Spectral Basis Unmixing and Structured Noise Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08777)
*Ziyi Guan,Jianping Zhang,Zheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AXS-Net，一种将高光谱图像去噪建模为低秩光谱子空间重构加结构化稀疏噪声加残差高斯噪声的优化问题，并通过K阶段交替近端点框架展开成深度学习网络，同时获得可解释的端元、丰度图和结构化噪声估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像常被混合噪声（带相关高斯噪声和结构化伪影如条纹、死线、脉冲噪声）退化。现有深度去噪方法直接回归干净图像，将信号与结构化噪声纠缠在一起，缺乏可解释性和对噪声结构的显式建模。

Method: 将HSI去噪建模为Y=AX+S+N，其中AX是低秩光谱子空间（解混）重构，S是结构化稀疏噪声，N是残差高斯噪声。将得到的正则化优化问题展开为AXS-Net，一个K阶段交替近端点框架。每个阶段包含：解析光谱基梯度步、用于丰度系数的SSX-Block近端算子、以及用于结构化残差的SBlock近端算子（具列一致和稀疏先验）。

Result: 在ICVL、CAVE和Harvard数据集及五种噪声配置上，AXS-Net实现了强域内精度和有竞争力的零样本迁移，在ICVL和Harvard的所有五种噪声条件下均有一致的性能提升。恢复的结构化噪声与合成参考高度吻合，恢复的光谱基平滑且按波段有序而非任意潜在通道。

Conclusion: 通过优化-展开对应关系，AXS-Net不仅实现了优异的去噪性能，还提供了可解释的端元、丰度图和结构化噪声估计，证明将物理模型融入深度网络架构的有效性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral images (HSIs) are often degraded by mixed noise, including band-dependent Gaussian perturbations and structured artifacts such as stripes, dead-lines, and impulse noise. Most deep denoisers regress the clean image directly, entangling signal and structured noise. We instead model HSI denoising as $\Y=\A\X+\Snoise+\Nnoise$, where $\A\X$ is a low-rank spectral-subspace (unmixing) reconstruction, $\Snoise$ is structured sparse noise and $\Nnoise$ is residual Gaussian noise. The resulting regularized optimization problem is unrolled into AXS-Net, a $K$-stage alternating proximal-point framework. Each stage combines an analytic spectral-basis gradient step, an SSX-Block proximal operator for abundance coefficients, and an SBlock proximal operator for the structured residual with column-consistent and sparse priors. This optimization correspondence exposes interpretable endmembers, abundance maps, and structured-noise estimates. Across ICVL, CAVE, and Harvard datasets and five noise configurations, the proposed AXS-Net achieves strong in-domain accuracy and competitive zero-shot transfer, with consistent gains across all five noise regimes on ICVL and Harvard. The recovered structured-noise closely follows the synthetic reference, and the recovered spectral basis is smooth and band-ordered rather than an arbitrary set of latent channels.

</details>


### [260] [Interpretable Hyperspectral Unmixing Framework with Fixed Endmember Prior and Structured Residual Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08786)
*Ziyi Guan,Jianping Zhang,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的分阶段高光谱解混框架(I-HyperSU)，在固定端元先验下，将解混过程显式分解为端元矩阵A、丰度块X和结构残差精炼块S。X块使用FISTA进行丰度估计（带非负性、稀疏性和软和为一约束），S块联合低秩SVD结构正则化和轻量深度图像先验(DIP)精炼残差。实验表明，在固定且不完美的端元先验下，软丰度松弛优于硬单纯形投影，重构误差降低61.7%-69.5%，且不牺牲丰度估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高光谱解混的模块化感知流程中，端元先被识别并固定，再进行丰度估计。当固定的端元先验不准确时，由光照变化、传感器伪影或物质边界引起的空间结构失配会被错误地归入丰度变量，导致分解不稳定。现有方法缺乏对这种结构性模型失配的显式建模和可解释处理。

Method: 提出I-HyperSU框架，将解混分解为三个显式块：(1)固定端元矩阵A；(2)丰度块X，使用FISTA求解，包含非负性、稀疏性增强以及近似满足和为一约束的软惩罚；(3)结构残差精炼块S，联合应用低秩SVD结构正则化和轻量DIP来精炼结构化残差。这种分阶段设计使丰度与残差分量之间的交互透明且可解释。

Result: 在Samson、Urban和Jasper Ridge数据集上的实验表明：在固定且不完美的端元先验下，软丰度松弛始终优于硬单纯形投影。在默认N-FINDR端元先验下，相比固定A的UCLS基线，联合重构误差降低61.7%-69.5%，而丰度RMSE几乎不变。Urban数据集上重构SAM从仅X模型的5.99°降至完整模型的1.92°。

Conclusion: 残差精炼分支能够捕获结构化模型失配，而不会损害丰度估计精度。将结构残差作为显式可解释的分块建模，比将误差全部归因于丰度变量更能稳定高光谱解混过程。

Abstract: Hyperspectral unmixing decomposes mixed pixels into material endmembers and their abundances from contiguous spectral observations. In modular sensing pipelines, endmembers are often first identified and then treated as fixed during abundance estimation. When this fixed endmember prior is inaccurate, spatially structured mismatch arising from illumination changes, sensor artifacts, or material boundaries may be incorrectly captured by the abundance variables, leading to unstable decompositions. This study presents an interpretable stage-wise hyperspectral unmixing framework (I-HyperSU) under fixed endmember priors, which is explicitly decomposed into a fixed endmember matrix $\mathbf{A}$, an abundance block $\mathbf{X}$, and a structural residual refinement block $\mathbf{S}$. The X-block estimates abundances using FISTA with nonnegativity and sparsity enhancement, and a soft penalty that approximately enforces sum-to-one constraints. The S-block jointly applies low-rank SVD structural regularization and a lightweight deep image prior (DIP) to refine structured residuals. This staged design makes the interaction between abundance and residual components transparent and interpretable. Experiments on Samson, Urban, and Jasper Ridge datasets demonstrate that, under fixed and imperfect endmember priors, soft abundance relaxation consistently outperforms hard simplex projection. Under the default N-FINDR endmember prior, the proposed framework reduces the joint reconstruction error by 61.7\%--69.5\% compared with a fixed-$\mathbf{A}$ UCLS baseline, while keeping the abundance RMSE nearly unchanged, indicating that the residual refinement branch accounts for structured model mismatch without degrading the abundance estimates. For example, on Urban, the reconstruction SAM decreases from $5.99^\circ$ for the X-only model to $1.92^\circ$ for the full model.

</details>


### [261] [Hi-FLoop: Hierarchical State-Feedback Loops for Multi-Timescale World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08796)
*Rx Fan,Zhan H*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HI-FLOOP是一个分支一致的多时间尺度状态反馈框架，用于多智能体长时域交通仿真。它通过场景级Worlds表示联合假设，在8秒rollout中引入1/2/0.5秒的层次化决策结构（Goal/Preview/Control），并通过0.5秒commit反馈仅执行的前缀。JPI机制利用稀疏有向未来图调控交互，前缀冻结的A-to-B级联用于生成状态恢复。在H-D测试集上Overall达0.689987，oracle-minADE@8达到1.196m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体交通仿真的长时域闭环生成需要在演化上下文中协调多个决策时间尺度。现有方法常从初始场景整体展开长时未来，将意图、交互和运动单一化处理，削弱了跨尺度一致性和自适应性。

Method: 1) 场景级Worlds：8个联合假设分支，所有智能体共享分支身份贯穿8秒。2) 多时间尺度分层：8秒Goal锚定意图、2秒Preview协调交互、1秒Control生成物理运动。3) 0.5秒commit反馈机制：仅执行前缀进入事实记忆，未执行假设不污染记忆。4) JPI（联合预览交互）：从Preview构造稀疏有向未来图，用冲突概率与符号到达时间差门控交互细化。5) 前缀冻结A-to-B级联：模型A生成0-1秒后，将类型化物理状态、上下文与分支索引（不含潜在状态）传给独立模型B，用于1-2秒恢复。

Result: 在H-D公共验证集955个场景上，单次S1运行Overall得分为0.689987（官方评估器）。在agent-centric oracle评估下，HI-FLOOP在8秒水平上实现oracle-minADE@8=1.196636m，6秒水平达0.526m。

Conclusion: HI-FLOOP通过分支一致的多时间尺度状态反馈框架，有效解决了长时域闭环生成中跨尺度一致性与状态自适应问题，实验验证了其在多样化、协调且物理真实的未来预测上的良好性能。

Abstract: Multi-agent traffic simulation seeks diverse, coordinated, and physically realistic futures from maps and observed history. Long-horizon closed-loop generation must reconcile multiple decision time scales while its context evolves with generated states. Existing methods often unfold long futures from the initial scene and resolve intent, interaction, and motion monolithically, weakening cross-scale consistency and adaptation. We present HI-FLOOP, a branch-consistent multi-timescale state-feedback framework. Eight scene-level Worlds represent joint hypotheses, and all agents share the selected World identity throughout an 8-second rollout. Within the branch, an 8-second Goal anchors intent, a 2-second Preview coordinates interactions, and 1-second Control produces physical motion. Every 0.5-second commit feeds back only its executed prefix as new facts, while unexecuted hypotheses never enter factual memory. Joint Preview Interaction (JPI) induces a sparse directed future graph from Preview and uses conflict probabilities and signed arrival-time differences to gate interaction refinement. For generated-state recovery, a prefix-frozen A-to-B cascade lets frozen Model A generate 0-1 seconds, then transfers typed physical state, admissible context, and the branch index, but no latent state, to an independent Model B for re-encoding and 1-2-second recovery. On the full H-D public-validation split of 955 scenarios, one complete S1 run yields an Overall score of 0.689987 with the official evaluator. Under agent-centric oracle evaluation, HI-FLOOP achieves oracle-minADE@8 of 1.196636 m over the 8-second horizon and 0.526 m over the 6-second horizon.

</details>


### [262] [Evaluation Principles for MRI-MRA Registration in Trigeminal Neuralgia: An ROI-Centered Neurovascular Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08805)
*Xupeng Zhang,Xihang Wang,Michael Xie,Haoyuan Liang,Hau Ern Lien,Oishika Das,James Feghali,Risheng Xu,Peirong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对三叉神经痛（TN）的MRI-MRA融合配准问题，提出将其建模为以ROI为中心的神经血管配准评估任务，并构建了149名患者、双侧三叉神经ROI标注的基准数据集。通过对比六种代表性配准流程，采用多种局部指标评估后发现：传统全局配准评估方法具有误导性，可变形精炼相对于仿射对齐仅带来微小且依赖FOV的改善，局部血管距离优势可能与全局不合理的配准并存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TN术前评估需要联合解读结构MRI（显示三叉神经及周围脑池解剖）和TOF-MRA（突出血管结构），MRI-MRA融合具有临床吸引力。但该任务无法被传统全脑配准评估方法有效捕获，因为临床相关目标是小的三叉神经ROI、血管标注是部分且临床聚焦的、局部TOF-MRA对比度可变、视野不匹配限制可变形对齐。

Method: 将TN MRI-MRA融合建模为ROI中心的神经血管配准-评估问题；构建149名患者双侧重标注ROI的基准；评估六种代表性配准流程，使用局部图像指标、分割衍生的血管定位指标、预测体积分析和对比度与FOV分层比较；并引入放射科医生审阅。

Result: 常规评估总结常具误导性：局部图像相似度、血管-背景可分性和下游血管定位不协同排序方法；单侧血管距离在部分标注下受预测血管范围强烈影响；局部MRA对比度决定血管可分性指标何时有效。可变形精炼仅比仿射对齐提供微小的FOV依赖改善，且医生审阅表明局部有利的血管距离可能与全局不合理的配准并存。

Conclusion: TN MRI-MRA配准应作为局部、血管感知、对比度敏感且FOV感知的可视化任务来评估，而非通用的多模态脑配准问题。代码已公开。

Abstract: Preoperative evaluation of trigeminal neuralgia (TN) often requires joint interpretation of structural MRI, which depicts the trigeminal nerve and surrounding cisternal anatomy, and time-of-flight MRA, which highlights vascular structures. Although MRI-MRA fusion is clinically attractive for visualizing neurovascular compression, this task is poorly captured by conventional whole-brain registration evaluation because the clinically relevant target is a small trigeminal ROI, vessel annotations are partial and clinically focused, local TOF-MRA contrast is variable, and field-of-view mismatch can limit deformable alignment. We formulate TN MRI-MRA fusion as an ROI-centered neurovascular registration-evaluation problem and construct a benchmark from 149 patients with clinician-annotated bilateral trigeminal ROIs. Six representative registration pipelines were evaluated using local image-based metrics, segmentation-derived vessel-localization metrics, prediction-volume analysis, and contrast- and FOV-stratified comparisons. Conventional evaluation summaries were often misleading: local image similarity, vessel-background separability, and downstream vessel localization did not co-rank methods; one-sided vessel distances were strongly affected by predicted vessel extent under partial annotations; and local MRA contrast determined when vessel-separability metrics were informative. Deformable refinement provided only a small, FOV-dependent benefit over affine alignment, while reader review showed that locally favorable vessel distances could coexist with globally implausible registrations. These findings indicate that TN MRI-MRA registration should be evaluated as a local, vessel-aware, contrast-sensitive, and FOV-aware visualization task rather than as generic multimodal brain registration. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/jhuldr/TN-Reg-Benchmark.

</details>


### [263] [ArmPoser: Real-Time, Calibration-Free Arm Pose Estimation from Smartwatch IMU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08806)
*Bishnu Dev,Vasco Xu,Xi-Aan Loh,Chenfeng Gao,Henry Hoffmann,Karan Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ArmPoser，一种基于单只智能手表IMU的无校准手臂姿态估计系统。核心思路是直接在智能手表的设备原生参考系中训练模型，消除对手腕方位校准、坐标变换和骨骼偏移校准的需求，并通过物理增强和佩戴配置模块处理用户差异。在公开数据集及10名参与者、30项活动的跨平台（watchOS和Android）研究中，ArmPoser在无需用户校准的情况下达到或超过校准基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能手表的手臂姿态估计方法依赖校准姿态和预处理流程来将原始IMU测量值转换为标准化训练格式，这些步骤阻碍了日常场景部署，且会因不完美校准和传感器漂移引入误差。

Method: 1) 直接在与消费级智能手表原生参考系中对齐的训练框架下训练模型；2) 采用基于物理的增强手段，模拟手表佩戴位置和手臂形态变化；3) 引入穿戴配置模块，自动推断前臂前后位置及表冠朝向。

Result: 在公开基准以及包含10名参与者、30项活动的watchOS与Android智能手表研究中，ArmPoser无需用户校准即可达到或超过经过校准的基线方法的性能。

Conclusion: 在设备原生坐标系中训练模型可以有效消除校准流程，实现免校准、用户友好的手臂姿态估计，适用于日常场景的跨平台部署。

Abstract: Arm pose estimation enables applications in fitness, extended reality input, rehabilitation, and life logging. Prior smartwatch-based approaches rely on calibration poses and preprocessing pipelines that transform raw IMU measurements into standardized training formats. These steps hinder deployment in everyday settings and introduce errors due to imperfect calibration and sensor drift. We present ArmPoser, a calibration-free arm pose estimation system using a single smartwatch IMU. Our central contribution is training models directly in the reference frame native to consumer smartwatches, aligning learning with how IMU data is produced by deployed devices. By operating on device-native axes, ArmPoser removes the need for coordinate transformations, explicit alignment, and bone-offset calibration used in prior work. We further augment training with physically grounded variations in watch placement and arm morphology to account for user-specific variability. ArmPoser also includes a wear-configuration module that infers anterior or posterior forearm placement and crown orientation. We evaluate pose estimation on public benchmarks and on a 10-participant, 30-activity study using watchOS and Android smartwatches, where ArmPoser matches or exceeds calibrated baselines without any user calibration.

</details>


### [264] [Beyond Gait: Person Identification from Millimeter-Wave Point Clouds Across Activities of Daily Living](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08818)
*Xilai Wang,Zixiong Han,Saad Rhanmouni,Chenzhe Zhao,Yunze Lu,Miodrag Bolic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用毫米波点云在不同日常生活活动（ADLs）中进行身份识别。作者提出了mm-ADL数据集（11名受试者、7种ADLs），并设计了活动条件化的专家混合框架（DS-SDPNet），利用人类活动识别路由器将每个片段分派给特定活动的身份专家。实验表明，硬路由将闭集识别准确率从62.1%提升至68.0%，在双人重识别场景下mAP从57.2%提升至75.4%，Rank-1从59.1%提升至82.1%，证明了ADLs可作为步态之外的补充身份信息来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于毫米波点云的人体识别主要依赖步态（gait），但室内行走往往短暂且常被打断。作者希望通过探索其他日常生活活动（ADLs）来提供互补的身份信息，弥补步态识别在室内场景中的局限性。

Method: 1) 构建mm-ADL数据集：11名受试者、7种ADLs的受控协议点云数据；2) 提出活动条件化框架：由人类活动识别路由器将每个片段分派到特定活动的身份专家；3) 实现为监督式专家混合（Mixture of Experts）模型；4) 使用双流静态-动态PointNet（DS-SDPNet）结合时间聚合的空间结构与帧间动态信息。

Result: 在闭集识别任务中，硬路由将准确率从62.1%提升到68.0%；在双人重识别（ReID）场景中，硬路由将mAP从57.2%提升到75.4%，Rank-1准确率从59.1%提升到82.1%；在匹配图库划分下，活动特定专家也优于共享嵌入，表明增益不仅来自图库缩小。

Conclusion: 研究验证了在受控室内条件下，利用ADLs（超越步态）进行身份识别的可行性，并证明了活动条件化学习的价值。

Abstract: Person identification from millimeter-wave (mmWave) point clouds has mainly relied on gait. Indoor walking, however, is often brief and interrupted, while other activities of daily living (ADLs) may provide complementary identity information. We investigate identification across seven ADLs using mm-ADL, a new point-cloud dataset collected from 11 subjects under a controlled protocol. This extension introduces heterogeneous states and transitions whose spatial and temporal characteristics vary with activity. We therefore study whether activity can provide useful context for learning identity representations. We propose an activity-conditioned framework in which a human activity recognition router dispatches each clip to an activity-specific identity expert. The framework is implemented as a supervised mixture of experts, using a dual-stream static-dynamic PointNet (DS-SDPNet) to combine time-aggregated spatial structure with frame-to-frame information. We evaluate closed-set identification (ID) and subject-disjoint re-identification (ReID). With learned hard routing, ID accuracy increases from 62.1% to 68.0%. In a two-occupant ReID setting, hard routing increases mAP from 57.2% to 75.4% and Rank-1 accuracy from 59.1% to 82.1%. Under a matched gallery partition, activity-specific experts also outperform a shared embedding, showing that the gain extends beyond restricting the gallery. These results support the feasibility of using ADLs beyond gait for identification and the value of activity conditioning under controlled indoor conditions.

</details>


### [265] [Leveraging Visual and Geometric Priors for Metric-scale and Complete Vehicle Gaussian Reconstruction from Limited Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08841)
*Jinyu Miao,Jiusi Li,Yifei He,Miao Long,Kun Jiang,Mengmeng Yang,Diange Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前馈的车辆资产重建方法，利用两种互补先验（视觉基础模型和车辆对称性）从稀疏单侧车载观测中重建具有度量尺度的3D高斯车辆表示，显著提升了车辆资产的完整性和几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量车辆资产对于可控交通场景生成至关重要，尤其是合成罕见的安全关键长尾场景。然而，从车载图像中重建可复用的车辆表示面临两大挑战：一是图像到3D生成方法缺乏可靠的度量尺度；二是车载相机通常只能观测到车辆单侧，导致常规多视角重建在不被观测区域不完整。

Method: 提出一种前馈式车辆资产重建方法：首先利用视觉基础模型作为视觉先验进行高斯初始化；随后通过可学习的编码器-解码器模块估计高斯属性；最后提出对称感知克隆策略，利用车辆的左右对称结构作为几何先验，直接在3D高斯空间中补全未观测侧。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法在车辆资产完整性和几何精度方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用视觉先验和几何先验（车辆对称性）这两种互补先验，成功解决了从稀疏单侧观察中重建具有度量尺度且完整的车辆3D表示这一难题。

Abstract: High-fidelity vehicle assets are essential for controllable traffic scene generation, particularly for synthesizing rare and safety-critical long-tail scenarios. However, reconstructing a reusable vehicle representation from in-the-wild onboard images remains challenging for two reasons. First, image-to-3D generation methods generally produce models without reliable metric scale. Second, onboard cameras usually observe only one side of a target vehicle, making conventional multi-view reconstruction incomplete on unobserved regions. To solve these problems, we propose a feed-forward vehicle asset reconstruction method, which leverages two complementary priors to reconstruct 3D Gaussian representations for vehicles using sparse one-sided observations. To achieve metric-scale reconstruction, a visual foundation model is first utilized to serve as a visual prior for Gaussian initialization. The Gaussian attributes are then estimated by a learnable encoder-decoder module. A symmetry-aware cloning strategy is presented to complete the unobserved side directly in Gaussian space, which exploits the bilateral structure of vehicles as a geometric prior. Experiments on the public dataset demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms existing approaches in both vehicle asset completeness and geometric accuracy.

</details>


### [266] [FIRE3D: Feed-forward Interactive 3D Scene Reconstruction Within A Minute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08848)
*Hongchi Xia,Tianhang Cheng,Wei-Chiu Ma,Shenlong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FIRE3D是一个统一框架，可从单张RGB图像或RGB视频在不到一分钟内生成可直接用于游戏和交互应用的三维场景资产。其核心是一个前馈端到端网络，从RGB捕获中估计的RGB-D观测中预测组合式场景表示，为每个物体输出6自由度姿态、包围盒、网格和纹理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么需要测试时优化导致速度慢，要么缺乏物体级完整性，难以直接应用于游戏和交互场景中的物理模拟。作者旨在构建一个无需逐样本优化的快速、可交互且组合式的场景重建框架。

Method: 采用前馈端到端网络架构，将场景建模为离散实体集合，从估计的RGB-D观测中预测每个物体的6-DoF姿态、包围盒、网格和纹理，从而生成物理解耦、模态完整且可交互的三维场景。

Result: 在多个数据集上，FIRE3D在姿态精度、几何完整性和纹理质量方面达到有竞争力或最先进的水平，同时比先前的交互就绪方法快数量级。

Conclusion: FIRE3D证明了无需测试时优化即可实现快速、模拟就绪的3D场景生成，为实时交互应用提供了高效且完整的场景资产解决方案。

Abstract: We present FIRE3D, a unified framework that takes a single RGB image or casual RGB video and transforms it into simulation-ready 3D scene assets for games and interactive applications in under a minute. At the core of FIRE3D is a feed-forward, end-to-end network that predicts a compositional scene representation from posed RGB-D observations estimated from the RGB capture, including the 6-DoF pose, bounding box, mesh, and texture for every object. By modeling the scene as a collection of discrete entities, FIRE3D produces amodally complete and simulation-ready environments where objects are physically decoupled and ready for interaction. Our framework requires no test-time optimization, runs orders of magnitude faster than prior interaction-ready methods, and provides object-level completeness beyond existing feed-forward 3D approaches. We demonstrate competitive or state-of-the-art results across pose accuracy, geometry completeness, and texture quality across various datasets while being orders of magnitudes faster. Project page: https://xiahongchi.github.io/Fire3D/

</details>


### [267] [DSE-VTG: Dual-Side Enhancement for Training-Free Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08850)
*Zhuo Cao,Bingqing Zhang,Sen Wang,Xue Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DSE-VTG，一个无需任务特定训练的视频时间定位（VTG）框架，通过视觉侧的多尺度相似性融合（MSF）和文本侧的查询级测试时自适应（Q-TTA）分别解决帧级视觉编码忽略时间动态和固定查询嵌入无法消解歧义两个信息瓶颈，在标准和OOD基准上均达到训练-free方法的最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练-free VTG方法虽然避免了密集时间标注和任务特定模型训练的成本，但仍面临两个信息瓶颈：1）帧级视觉编码忽略了视频的时间动态信息；2）固定不变的查询嵌入无法解决文本查询的模糊性问题。

Method: 提出DSE-VTG双端增强框架：1）视觉侧采用多尺度相似性融合（MSF），将帧级和片段级相似度融合为统一的、具有时间感知的相似度图谱；2）文本侧采用查询级测试时自适应（Q-TTA），通过优化轻量级的加性偏移量，在测试时将查询嵌入自适应到视频内容，无需微调骨干网络或调用外部大语言模型。

Result: 在三个标准基准和两个OOD基准上，DSE-VTG达到训练-free方法中的最先进水平。在Charades-STA上比最强的训练-free方法mIoU提升5.61个点；在分布偏移场景Charades-CG Novel-Word上达到50.86 mIoU，超过最强监督基线2.76点。

Conclusion: 通过同时在视觉和文本两侧进行增强，DSE-VTG无需训练即可有效应对时间动态缺失和查询歧义问题，在标准及分布偏移场景下展现出优越性，验证了双端增强策略在训练-free VTG任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Text-guided Video Temporal Grounding (VTG) aims to localize the relevant segments in an untrimmed video based on text queries, yet collecting dense temporal annotations and training task-specific models remain costly and brittle under distribution shift. Recent training-free VTG approaches mitigate this issue by directly matching pretrained vision-language representations, but they still face two fundamental information bottlenecks: frame-wise visual encoding overlooks temporal dynamics, while fixed query embeddings cannot resolve query ambiguity. To address these issues, we propose DSE-VTG, a \underline{D}ual-\underline{S}ide \underline{E}nhancement framework that addresses both without any task-specific training. On the visual side, Multi-scale Similarity Fusion (MSF) combines frame- and clip-level similarities into a unified, temporally aware similarity profile. On the textual side, Query-level Test-Time Adaptation (Q-TTA) optimizes a lightweight additive offset to adapt the query embedding to the video at test time, without finetuning the backbone or calling external large language models. Extensive experiments on three standard and two OOD benchmarks show that DSE-VTG achieves state-of-the-art performance among training-free methods. On Charades-STA, it improves mIoU over the strongest prior training-free method by 5.61 points. Under distribution shift, DSE-VTG reaches 50.86 mIoU on Charades-CG Novel-Word, surpassing the strongest supervised baseline by 2.76 mIoU. Our code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [268] [FRAME: Factored Retrieval via Attribute Readouts for Object-Centric Scene Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08886)
*Woosang Jeon,Sanghyeok Choi,Minwoo Kim,Taehyun Jung,Taehyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出属性组合检索（attribute-compositional retrieval）问题，针对语言引导机器人需要持久场景记忆中按多属性检索物体的需求。作者提出FRAME方法，将语言转化为查询相关的属性权重，从物体嵌入中估计各属性的证据，并按查询聚合排序。实验表明FRAME在固定场景记忆和属性定义目标上优于现有基线，且计算高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导机器人需要持久场景记忆来跟随指令、复访物体和解析指代。现有语言引导场景记忆检索多强调空间或关系引用，但日常中的物体引用常通过多个持久属性（如类别、材料、尺寸、外观）来指定。需要直接研究这种属性组合检索能力，并将其与感知和标注歧义分离。

Method: 提出FRAME框架：1）将自然语言转化为查询相关的属性权重；2）使用学习到的readout从物体嵌入中估计每个属性的证据；3）根据查询将这些证据聚合以对物体进行排序。同时设计了固定场景记忆和属性定义目标的受控评估协议，将检索与感知、标注歧义分离。

Result: 在未见过的场景和物体资产上，FRAME优于代表性的场景记忆检索基线，并将分解后的物体评分简化为轻量级的矩阵-向量计算，在保持准确性的同时提高了效率。

Conclusion: 属性组合检索可作为语言引导机器人的补充场景记忆能力，持久物体属性可被暴露为可组合的证据，以实现准确且高效的多属性检索。

Abstract: Language-guided robots need persistent scene memories to follow instructions, revisit objects, and resolve references to objects encountered over time. While much of language-guided scene-memory retrieval has emphasized spatial or relational references, many everyday object references specify objects by multiple persistent attributes, such as category, material, size, or surface appearance. We formalize this problem as attribute-compositional retrieval, where a fixed object-centric scene memory is queried with natural language to retrieve the object satisfying the requested attributes. To investigate this capability directly, we introduce a controlled evaluation protocol with fixed scene memories and attribute-defined targets, separating retrieval from perception and annotation ambiguities. We then propose FRAME, which turns language into query-relevant attribute weights, uses learned readouts to estimate per-attribute evidence from object embeddings, and ranks objects by aggregating this evidence according to the query. Across held-out scenes and object assets, FRAME outperforms representative scene-memory retrieval baselines while reducing post-decomposition object scoring to lightweight matrix-vector computation. These results position attribute-compositional retrieval as a complementary scene-memory capability for language-guided robots, showing that persistent object attributes can be exposed as composable evidence for accurate and efficient multi-attribute retrieval.

</details>


### [269] [Spheriverse: 3D Scene Understanding from Spherical Observations in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09012)
*Fei Teng,Sheng Wu,Mengfei Duan,Guoqiang Zhao,Junhui Ma,Kai Luo,Siyu Li,Hao Shi,Zhiyong Li,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Spheriverse数据集（64,400对时间对齐的球面图像-激光雷达数据，共644个序列），覆盖多样场景和天气条件，并建立语义占用预测、语义建图和3D目标检测三大基准，评估30+方法。同时提出SphereOcc占用预测框架，通过Cartesian-Spherical表示重构（CSRR）和球面证据再查询（SER）模块实现跨空间表示的对齐，在mIoU和GeoIoU上分别达到13.91%和24.65%，超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面观测为3D场景理解提供全局视觉上下文，但视觉信息编码在角度域而物理世界用笛卡尔坐标表示，这种跨空间表示差距阻碍了几何对应和语义证据聚合。现有方法缺乏处理该间隙的有效手段，且缺乏大规模球面图像与激光雷达的配对数据集来支撑相关研究。

Method: 1) 构建Spheriverse大规模数据集（64,400对球面图像-激光雷达，644个序列），覆盖多样场景、光照和天气，含细粒度语义类别。2) 提出SphereOcc占用框架：CSRR模块将球面距离-方位角几何通过区域级调制融入笛卡尔体素特征；SER模块基于体素内容和距离-高度-方位角几何自适应检索球面图像特征中的相关语义证据。

Result: SphereOcc在mIoU达到13.91%、GeoIoU达到24.65%，分别超过最优方法TPVFormer和SurroundOcc达1.70和2.10个百分点。在全部5个场景中均排名第一，并在不同空间分区和缩小视野下保持一致的性能优势。

Conclusion: 球面图像与笛卡尔坐标间的跨空间表示差距可以通过显式几何耦合与证据检索机制有效弥合，所提出的数据集和SphereOcc框架为该方向奠定了坚实基础，相关基准和代码将公开。

Abstract: Spherical observations provide global visual context for 3D scene understanding. However, visual information is encoded in an angular domain, whereas the physical world is represented in Cartesian coordinates. This cross-space representation gap complicates geometric correspondence and semantic evidence aggregation. To delve into this challenge, we introduce Spheriverse, comprising $64,400$ temporally aligned spherical image-LiDAR pairs organized into 644 sequences. The dataset spans diverse scenes, illumination, and weather conditions, with fine-grained semantic classes. We further establish benchmarks for semantic occupancy prediction, semantic mapping, and 3D object detection, evaluating 30+ methods through overall and scene-wise comparisons. For dense prediction, we propose SphereOcc, an occupancy framework that couples spherical geometry modeling with semantic evidence retrieval. Cartesian-Spherical Representation Remodeling (CSRR) incorporates spherical range-azimuth geometry into Cartesian voxel features through region-wise modulation. Spherical Evidence Re-querying (SER) then conditions queries on voxel content and range-height-azimuth geometry to adaptively retrieve relevant semantic evidence from source spherical image features. SphereOcc achieves 13.91% mIoU and 24.65% GeoIoU, outperforming the respective best-performing methods, TPVFormer and SurroundOcc, by 1.70 and 2.10 percentage points. It also ranks first in both metrics across all five scenes, with consistent advantages across the evaluated spatial partitions and reduced fields of view. The established benchmark and source code will be available at https://feit-feiteng.github.io/Spheriverse.

</details>


### [270] [Mask Forcing: Improving Autoregressive Video Diffusion Distillation via Dual-Noise Masking Rollout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09123)
*Zhuoran Zhao,Shengju Qian,Tongtong Liang,Xianghao Kong,Songchun Zhang,Junchao Huang,Guian Fang,Xin Wang,Pan Hui,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mask Forcing方法，通过双噪声掩码滚动策略来缓解自回归视频扩散模型在分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）过程中因反向KL散度的模式寻求行为导致的模式坍缩问题，从而提升生成视频的视觉质量和真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型虽具实时生成潜力，但通过DMD蒸馏得到的视频常出现过饱和和过平滑问题，视觉质量受限。根本原因是反向KL目标函数的模式寻求行为导致学生分布坍缩到教师分布的少数模式上。

Method: 提出Mask Forcing策略，即双噪声掩码滚动方法。在自回归扩散蒸馏的自滚动过程中，沿空间和时间轴随机注入掩码，将更干净的信号注入到含噪的滚动输入中。这种扰动鼓励学生探索教师分布的更广阔区域，同时干净的token为其他含噪token提供去噪引导，减少误差累积。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法能在不引入真实视频数据或额外后期训练阶段的情况下，有效提升多种自回归视频扩散蒸馏方法的视觉质量与效率。

Conclusion: Mask Forcing通过扰动自回归学生自滚动过程来对抗反向KL模式寻求引起的模式坍缩，是一种通用且高效的改进策略，可广泛应用于多种自回归视频扩散蒸馏方法。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models have shown great potential in real-time video generation. Recent methods distill pretrained bidirectional video diffusion models into causal AR students through Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD), but the generated videos often suffer from over-saturation and over-smoothing issues, resulting in limited visual quality and realism. The key contributing factor is the mode-seeking behavior of the reverse KL objective in DMD, which can cause the student distribution to collapse onto only a few modes of the teacher distribution. To address this, we propose Mask Forcing, a Dual-Noise Masking Rollout strategy that perturbs the AR student self-rollout to mitigate mode collapse induced by reverse-KL mode seeking. The core idea is to inject cleaner signals into noisy rollout inputs via random masks along spatial and temporal axes during the self-rollout process of AR diffusion distillation. Such perturbations encourage the student rollouts to explore more regions of the teacher distribution, allowing DMD to provide learning signals beyond the modes already covered by the student. Moreover, the cleaner tokens act as denoising guidance for other noisier tokens, improving the intermediate rollout predictions and reducing error accumulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method improves multiple AR video diffusion distillation methods with higher visual quality efficiently, without incorporating real video data or additional post-training stages.

</details>
